BoldEdge needs the best onboarding in coaching SaaS.\\\\n\\\\n### Coach Onboarding Flow (First Login)\\\\n\\\\n**Screen 1: \\\\\\\"What brings you here?\\\\\\\"**\\\\n- [ ] I'm an executive/business coach\\\\n- [ ] I run peer groups or masterminds\\\\n- [ ] I'm exploring coaching tools\\\\n- [ ] My colleague referred me\\\\n\\\\u2192 Routes to personalized setup based on answer\\\\n\\\\n**Screen 2: \\\\\\\"Let's set up your practice\\\\\\\" (60-second form)**\\\\n- Your name + photo\\\\n- Coaching specialty (executive, business, leadership, team, career)\\\\n- Number of active clients (0-5, 6-15, 16-30, 31+)\\\\n- Primary service (1:1, group, peer advisory, programs, mix)\\\\n\\\\u2192 Platform pre-configures dashboard, templates, and workflows based on answers\\\\n\\\\n**Screen 3: \\\\\\\"Add your first client\\\\\\\" (the activation moment)**\\\\n- Simple form: Client name, email, company name\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Send them a diagnostic?\\\\\\\" toggle (default ON)\\\\n- Client receives branded email invitation\\\\n- Coach sees: \\\\\\\"Diagnostic sent! You'll see results on your dashboard when they complete it.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\u2192 **This is the \\\\\\\"aha moment.\\\\\\\"** Coach sees the system working in real-time.\\\\n\\\\n**Screen 4: \\\\\\\"While you wait, take your own diagnostic\\\\\\\"**\\\\n- \\\\\\\"See how BoldEdge works from the client's perspective\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Coach takes the Business Health Check on their own coaching business\\\\n- Results appear in a demo dashboard\\\\n- \\\\\\\"This is what your client will see. Now imagine 10 clients, all tracked here.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n**Screen 5: \\\\\\\"You're live. Here's your next 3 steps.\\\\\\\"**\\\\n- \\\\u2705 Client invited (done)\\\\n- \\\\u2b1c Schedule your first session (calendar link or manual)\\\\n- \\\\u2b1c Customize your dashboard (pick which KPIs to track)\\\\n- Progress bar: \\\\\\\"You're 40% set up\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### Client Onboarding Flow (First Login)\\\\n\\\\n**Step 1:** Branded email from coach: \\\\\\\"Welcome to BoldEdge \\\\u2014 [Coach Name] has invited you\\\\\\\"\\\\n**Step 2:** Click \\\\u2192 lands on diagnostic (no login required for first diagnostic)\\\\n**Step 3:** Complete diagnostic (48 questions, ~10 minutes, progress bar, save & resume)\\\\n**Step 4:** Instant results page: Business Health Score + radar chart + top 3 recommendations\\\\n**Step 5:** \\\\\\\"Create your account to save results and access your dashboard\\\\\\\"\\\\n**Step 6:** Client dashboard with pre-populated data from diagnostic\\\\n\\\\n**Key principle:** Client gets VALUE before they create an account. The diagnostic is the product demo.\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 3. FREE TIER / FREEMIUM STRATEGY\\\\n\\\\n### What's Free Forever:\\\\n- 1 client\\\\n- Basic diagnostic (no benchmarking)\\\\n- 3 session notes\\\\n- Business Health Score\\\\n- 5 templates\\\\n- BoldEdge branding on all exports (\\\\\\\"Powered by BoldEdge\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n### What This Achieves:\\\\n- Every coach can test the core loop (invite client \\\\u2192 run diagnostic \\\\u2192 see dashboard) with zero risk\\\\n- Free users generate diagnostic data that feeds the benchmark network\\\\n- Free users see \\\\\\\"Unlock benchmarking\\\\\\\" / \\\\\\\"Add more clients\\\\\\\" upgrade prompts at natural friction points\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Powered by BoldEdge\\\\\\\" on free-tier exports = casual contact viral loop\\\\n\\\\n### Upgrade Triggers (natural, not annoying):\\\\n- Try to add 2nd client \\\\u2192 \\\\\\\"Upgrade to Starter for up to 10 clients\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Try to export without branding \\\\u2192 \\\\\\\"Remove BoldEdge branding on Professional\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Try to access 360\\\\u00b0 feedback \\\\u2192 \\\\\\\"Available on Professional and above\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Complete 3 months \\\\u2192 \\\\\\\"You've coached 1 client for 3 months. See your coaching effectiveness score \\\\u2014 available on Professional.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 4. CONTENT ENGINE (Built Into the Product)\\\\n\\\\n### SEO Content That Writes Itself:\\\\nBoldEdge's data generates content automatically:\\\\n\\\\n| Auto-Generated Content | SEO Target | Frequency |\\\\n|----------------------|-----------|-----------|\\\\n| \\\\\\\"Average Business Health Score by Industry\\\\\\\" | \\\\\\\"business health benchmark [industry]\\\\\\\" | Quarterly |\\\\n| \\\\\\\"Top 5 Gaps in [State] Small Businesses\\\\\\\" | \\\\\\\"small business challenges [state]\\\\\\\" | Quarterly |\\\\n| \\\\\\\"What the Best-Run Small Businesses Do Differently\\\\\\\" | \\\\\\\"how to improve business operations\\\\\\\" | Annual |\\\\n| \\\\\\\"Coaching ROI: Real Numbers from 500+ Engagements\\\\\\\" | \\\\\\\"executive coaching ROI\\\\\\\" | Annual |\\\\n| Template library pages | \\\\\\\"executive coaching session template\\\\\\\" | Ongoing |\\\\n| Framework guides | \\\\\\\"GROW model coaching questions\\\\\\\" | Evergreen |\\\\n\\\\n### Coach-Generated Content (UGC Loop):\\\\n- Coaches can publish anonymized case studies from their BoldEdge data\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Sarah improved her client's Operations score by 28 points in 6 months. Here's how.\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Auto-generated from platform data (with coach approval)\\\\n- Published on BoldEdge blog + coach's profile\\\\n- SEO + social proof + lead generation for the coach\\\\n\\\\n### Knowledge Base Within the Product:\\\\nNot a separate wiki \\\\u2014 embedded contextually:\\\\n- Mouse over \\\\\\\"Operations\\\\\\\" on any diagnostic \\\\u2192 tooltip: \\\\\\\"Operations measures your business processes, technology, delivery quality, and efficiency. Companies scoring 75+ grow 30% faster.\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Click any recommendation \\\\u2192 \\\\\\\"Why this matters\\\\\\\" section with data\\\\n- Every framework referenced includes a \\\\\\\"Learn more\\\\\\\" expandable with 2-3 paragraphs + source links\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Coaching Corner\\\\\\\" section: weekly article curated for coaches (best practices, industry trends, technique spotlights)\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 5. MOBILE EXPERIENCE\\\\n\\\\n### Coach Mobile App (Priorities):\\\\n1. **Session prep brief** \\\\u2014 swipe through client summary before a session (even in the elevator)\\\\n2. **Quick note capture** \\\\u2014 voice-to-text session notes, auto-tagged to client\\\\n3. **Task quick-add** \\\\u2014 assign a task to a client in 3 taps\\\\n4. **Notification center** \\\\u2014 overdue tasks, upcoming sessions, at-risk alerts\\\\n5. **Client quick-view** \\\\u2014 tap client name \\\\u2192 BHS score, last session, open tasks\\\\n6. **Offline mode** \\\\u2014 session notes save locally, sync when connected\\\\n\\\\n### Client Mobile App (Priorities):\\\\n1. **Business Health Score** \\\\u2014 big number, front and center, swipe for trend\\\\n2. **Weekly pulse check-in** \\\\u2014 push notification Monday 8 AM, complete in 30 seconds\\\\n3. **Task list** \\\\u2014 check off completed tasks, see what's due\\\\n4. **Session reminder** \\\\u2014 with pre-session self-assessment inline\\\\n5. **Win journal** \\\\u2014 quick capture, photo attachment, voice note\\\\n6. **Habit tracker** \\\\u2014 daily check-in with streak counter\\\\n\\\\n### Mobile-Specific Features:\\\\n- **Apple Watch complication** \\\\u2014 Business Health Score on watch face\\\\n- **Widget** \\\\u2014 iOS/Android home screen widget showing BHS + next session\\\\n- **Push notifications** \\\\u2014 smart timing based on user activity patterns\\\\n- **Siri/Google shortcut** \\\\u2014 \\\\\\\"Hey Siri, add a win to BoldEdge\\\\\\\" \\\\u2192 voice capture\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 6. TRUST, COMPLIANCE & CREDIBILITY SIGNALS\\\\n\\\\n### ICF Alignment:\\\\n- Diagnostic questions aligned with ICF Core Competencies\\\\n- ROI calculation follows ICF-recommended methodology\\\\n- Platform displays: \\\\\\\"BoldEdge follows ICF measurement guidelines\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Future: Apply for ICF Continuing Coach Education (CCE) credits for BoldEdge training\\\\n\\\\n### Security & Privacy:\\\\n- SOC 2 Type II compliance roadmap (not needed at launch, but signal intent)\\\\n- End-to-end encryption for session notes and client data\\\\n- Client data owned by coach \\\\u2014 if they leave, they export everything\\\\n- Data processing agreement (DPA) available for enterprise\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Your data is never shared. Period.\\\\\\\" prominently on settings page\\\\n- Two-factor authentication\\\\n- Session recording stored encrypted with auto-deletion options\\\\n\\\\n### Trust Signals on Every Page:\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Last backup: 2 minutes ago\\\\\\\" in footer\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Data encrypted at rest and in transit\\\\\\\" in security settings\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Served from [region] \\\\u2014 GDPR compliant\\\\\\\" for international coaches\\\\n- Client-facing: \\\\\\\"Your responses are confidential and shared only with your coach\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### Professional Credibility:\\\\n- Coach profile: display certifications (ICF ACC/PCC/MCC, DISC, EQ-i, StrengthsFinder)\\\\n- Verified badge: \\\\\\\"BoldEdge Verified Coach\\\\\\\" (completed training + has active clients)\\\\n- Testimonial display: verified outcome badges from the platform\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Member since [date]\\\\\\\" on profile\\\\n- Aggregate stats: \\\\\\\"Has coached 23 clients on BoldEdge with average 19-point BHS improvement\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 7. API & ECOSYSTEM PLAY\\\\n\\\\n### Public API (Business+ tier):\\\\n- RESTful API with full documentation\\\\n- Endpoints: clients, diagnostics, sessions, tasks, KPIs, reports\\\\n- Webhook events: diagnostic_completed, session_completed, task_completed, score_changed\\\\n- Use cases:\\\\n  - Custom dashboards in client's existing tools\\\\n  - Automated data pulls into BI platforms (Tableau, Looker, PowerBI)\\\\n  - CRM sync (push BoldEdge data to Salesforce/HubSpot)\\\\n  - Custom integrations coaches build themselves\\\\n\\\\n### Embeddable Widgets:\\\\n- **Diagnostic embed** \\\\u2014 coach puts diagnostic on their own website. Client completes it \\\\u2192 data flows to BoldEdge.\\\\n  - `<iframe src=\\\\\\\"boldedge.io/embed/diagnostic/[coach-id]\\\\\\\" />`\\\\n  - Branded with coach's logo and colors\\\\n  - This is a massive lead-gen tool. Every coach website becomes a BoldEdge acquisition channel.\\\\n- **Business Health Score badge** \\\\u2014 client embeds their score on their own site\\\\n  - Like a \\\\\\\"BBB Accredited\\\\\\\" badge but for business health\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"Business Health Score: 78 | Verified by BoldEdge\\\\\\\"\\\\n  - Click \\\\u2192 public profile with score trend (if client opts in)\\\\n  - Viral loop: other businesses see it and want their own score\\\\n\\\\n### Zapier/Make Integration:\\\\n- Triggers: New client, Diagnostic completed, Session completed, Task overdue, Score changed, Recommendation accepted\\\\n- Actions: Create client, Add KPI data point, Create task, Send notification\\\\n- Use cases:\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"When diagnostic is completed \\\\u2192 send Slack notification to coach\\\\\\\"\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"When task is overdue by 3 days \\\\u2192 send reminder via Twilio SMS\\\\\\\"\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"When new client added \\\\u2192 create Google Drive folder\\\\\\\"\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"When session completed \\\\u2192 log to Airtable tracking sheet\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 8. ADVANCED GAMIFICATION\\\\n\\\\nBeyond badges \\\\u2014 gamification that drives real behavioral change:\\\\n\\\\n### For Clients:\\\\n\\\\n**Levels System:**\\\\n- Level 1: Newcomer (completed intake)\\\\n- Level 2: Committed (completed first diagnostic)\\\\n- Level 3: In Motion (completed 3 sessions + 5 tasks)\\\\n- Level 4: Gaining Traction (BHS improved 10+ points)\\\\n- Level 5: Transforming (BHS improved 20+ points)\\\\n- Level 6: High Performer (BHS above 75)\\\\n- Level 7: Elite (BHS above 85 for 3+ months)\\\\n- Level 8: Champion (BHS above 90 + refers another client)\\\\n\\\\nEach level unlocks:\\\\n- A visual badge on their dashboard\\\\n- A shareable achievement graphic\\\\n- Access to progressively advanced resources\\\\n- At Level 5+: invitation to join a peer group of similar-level clients\\\\n\\\\n**Streaks:**\\\\n- Weekly pulse check-in streak: \\\\\\\"12 weeks in a row! \\\\ud83d\\\\udd25\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Task completion streak: \\\\\\\"Perfect month \\\\u2014 all tasks completed on time!\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Session attendance streak: \\\\\\\"6 consecutive sessions attended\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Broken streaks trigger gentle re-engagement: \\\\\\\"You missed your check-in this week. Take 30 seconds to get back on track.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n**Challenges:**\\\\n- Coach assigns a 30-day challenge: \\\\\\\"Delegate one decision per day for 30 days\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Client checks in daily\\\\n- Completion = badge + highlight in next session prep\\\\n- Peer group challenges: \\\\\\\"Which group member can improve their Financial score the most in 90 days?\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### For Coaches:\\\\n\\\\n**Coaching Milestones:**\\\\n- 10th client onboarded\\\\n- 100th session completed\\\\n- 50th diagnostic run\\\\n- First client reaches BHS 80+\\\\n- 5 clients with positive ROI verified\\\\n- 1,000 coaching hours logged\\\\n\\\\n**Leaderboard (opt-in, anonymized):**\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Your average client BHS improvement: 18 points (top 25% of BoldEdge coaches)\\\\\\\"\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Your client retention rate: 88% (top 15%)\\\\\\\"\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Your task completion rate: 85% (above average)\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Not competitive \\\\u2014 more like \\\\\\\"here's where you stand\\\\\\\" benchmarking\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 9. WHITE-LABEL DEEP MECHANICS\\\\n\\\\nFor Enterprise tier ($499-999/mo) \\\\u2014 what makes white-label actually valuable:\\\\n\\\\n### Visual Customization:\\\\n- Custom logo (replaces BoldEdge logo everywhere)\\\\n- Custom color scheme (primary, secondary, accent)\\\\n- Custom fonts\\\\n- Custom email templates (header, footer, colors)\\\\n- Custom domain: `coaching.mereditheicher.com`\\\\n- Custom favicon\\\\n- Removable \\\\\\\"Powered by BoldEdge\\\\\\\" footer\\\\n\\\\n### Content Customization:\\\\n- Custom diagnostic questions (add, remove, reword)\\\\n- Custom scoring weights\\\\n- Custom recommendation library\\\\n- Custom templates (replace defaults with coach's own)\\\\n- Custom onboarding flow text\\\\n- Custom email copy\\\\n\\\\n### Branding Deliverables:\\\\n- All PDF exports use coach's branding\\\\n- Client portal shows coach's branding only\\\\n- Proposals and agreements branded\\\\n- Diagnostic emails branded\\\\n- ROI reports branded\\\\n\\\\n### What BoldEdge Still Powers (Under the Hood):\\\\n- All infrastructure and hosting\\\\n- AI intelligence and benchmarking\\\\n- Workflow automation engine\\\\n- Data storage and security\\\\n- Platform updates and new features\\\\n- Support and maintenance\\\\n\\\\n**Pitch to coaches:** \\\\\\\"Your brand. Our brain. Give your clients a world-class technology experience with your name on it.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 10. COACH SUCCESS SYSTEM (Reduce Coach Churn)\\\\n\\\\nThe biggest risk to BoldEdge is coach churn. If coaches leave, everything stops. Build systems to make coaches successful:\\\\n\\\\n### Coach Onboarding Checklist (First 30 Days):\\\\n- [ ] Week 1: Complete profile, take personal diagnostic, add first client\\\\n- [ ] Week 2: Client completes diagnostic, coach reviews results, first session prep used\\\\n- [ ] Week 3: First post-session follow-up workflow runs, 2+ tasks assigned\\\\n- [ ] Week 4: Second client added OR second session with first client\\\\n- Progress tracked on \\\\\\\"Coach Success Dashboard\\\\\\\" (internal admin view)\\\\n\\\\n### Automated Coach Health Monitoring:\\\\n- **Green:** Coach has 3+ active clients, runs sessions weekly, uses diagnostic\\\\n- **Yellow:** Coach has 1-2 clients, sessions sporadic, some features unused\\\\n- **Red:** Coach hasn't logged in 14+ days, no sessions in 30 days\\\\n- Automated nudges for Yellow/Red:\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"You haven't run a diagnostic in 3 weeks. Want to send one to [client name]?\\\\\\\"\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"Most BoldEdge coaches see retention improve after using session prep briefs. Try it before your next session.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### Coach Community:\\\\n- Private forum/Slack for BoldEdge coaches\\\\n- Monthly webinar: \\\\\\\"BoldEdge Best Practices\\\\\\\" featuring a power user\\\\n- Template sharing: coaches contribute templates to a shared library\\\\n- Peer matching: connect coaches in non-competing markets for knowledge exchange\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Coach of the Month\\\\\\\" spotlight\\\\n\\\\n### Coach Training Resources:\\\\n- Video library: How to use every feature (2-3 min each)\\\\n- \\\\\\\"The BoldEdge Method\\\\\\\" \\\\u2014 a coaching methodology built around the diagnostic\\\\n  - Step 1: Diagnostic \\\\u2192 Baseline\\\\n  - Step 2: Priority Gap Identification \\\\u2192 Focus Areas\\\\n  - Step 3: Coaching Plan \\\\u2192 90-Day Objectives\\\\n  - Step 4: Session Cadence \\\\u2192 Weekly/Bi-weekly\\\\n  - Step 5: Task & Action Loop \\\\u2192 Between-Session Accountability\\\\n  - Step 6: Re-Diagnostic \\\\u2192 Measure Progress\\\\n  - Step 7: ROI Report \\\\u2192 Prove Value\\\\n  - Step 8: Renewal \\\\u2192 Continue Engagement\\\\n- Certification program (as described in Doc 06)\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 11. EVENT & OFFLINE INTEGRATION\\\\n\\\\nCoaching doesn't only happen online. BoldEdge should bridge digital and in-person:\\\\n\\\\n### Conference/Workshop Mode:\\\\n- Coach runs a workshop at a conference\\\\n- Audience scans QR code \\\\u2192 takes mini-diagnostic (10 questions) on phone\\\\n- Instant results on their phone\\\\n- Coach shows aggregate audience results on screen: \\\\\\\"This room's biggest gap is Strategy\\\\\\\"\\\\n- CTA: \\\\\\\"Want the full diagnostic? Connect with [coach name]\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Every attendee becomes a lead in BoldEdge CRM\\\\n\\\\n### Retreat Integration (expanded from Doc 06):\\\\n- **Pre-retreat survey** \\\\u2014 team members complete individual diagnostics\\\\n- **Live session tools:**\\\\n  - Polling: \\\\\\\"What's your team's #1 challenge?\\\\\\\" (live results on screen)\\\\n  - Breakout group assignment based on diagnostic profiles\\\\n  - Commitment capture: each person enters their 90-day commitment on phone\\\\n- **Post-retreat:**\\\\n  - All commitments tracked as tasks in BoldEdge\\\\n  - 30/60/90 day automated check-ins\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"Retreat Impact Report\\\\\\\" generated at 90 days\\\\n\\\\n### Speaking Engagement Lead Capture:\\\\n- Coach gives a talk \\\\u2192 references Business Health Score\\\\n- Audience texts keyword to number \\\\u2192 gets diagnostic link\\\\n- Or scans QR \\\\u2192 same flow\\\\n- All leads tracked in BoldEdge with source tag: \\\\\\\"Event: [Name] [Date]\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 12. AI CAPABILITIES \\\\u2014 NEXT LEVEL\\\\n\\\\nBeyond what's in Docs 05-07:\\\\n\\\\n### Voice Notes \\\\u2192 Structured Data:\\\\n- Coach records voice note after session on phone\\\\n- AI transcribes \\\\u2192 extracts: key themes, action items, mood assessment, follow-up questions\\\\n- Auto-files to correct client's session record\\\\n- Coach reviews and approves \\\\u2192 action items auto-created as tasks\\\\n\\\\n### AI Coaching Debrief Partner:\\\\n- After a session, coach can \\\\\\\"debrief\\\\\\\" with AI:\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"The session with James went off track. He kept avoiding the financial review discussion.\\\\\\\"\\\\n  - AI responds: \\\\\\\"This is the 3rd time James has avoided financials. His Financial diagnostic score is 38. Consider opening next session with: 'James, I've noticed a pattern...'\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Not replacing coaching supervision \\\\u2014 augmenting it with data-backed reflection\\\\n\\\\n### Predictive Client Matching:\\\\n- When a new prospect enters (via diagnostic or referral), AI suggests:\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"This prospect profiles similarly to your most successful clients (Sarah, Mark). Recommend 1:1 Premium engagement.\\\\\\\"\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"This prospect's profile matches clients who churn within 3 months at the Starter level. Consider a deeper onboarding.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### Natural Language KPI Entry:\\\\n- Client types: \\\\\\\"Revenue was $142K this month, up from $128K last month. Hired 2 new people.\\\\\\\"\\\\n- AI parses \\\\u2192 updates KPIs: Revenue: $142,000 (+10.9%), Headcount: +2\\\\n- Coach sees updated dashboard without manual data entry\\\\n\\\\n### AI-Generated Quarterly Business Review:\\\\n- Every 90 days, AI auto-generates a comprehensive review:\\\\n  - BHS trend\\\\n  - KPI changes\\\\n  - Tasks completed and impact\\\\n  - Diagnostic score changes\\\\n  - Sessions attended\\\\n  - Wins logged\\\\n  - Recommendations accepted and status\\\\n- Coach reviews, edits, and sends to client as a branded PDF\\\\n- Saves 2-3 hours per client per quarter\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 13. FINANCIAL MODELING SUITE (For Business-Focused Coaching)\\\\n\\\\nBeyond the cash runway calculator \\\\u2014 a light financial modeling layer:\\\\n\\\\n### Break-Even Calculator:\\\\n- Inputs: Fixed costs, variable costs, average revenue per unit\\\\n- Output: \\\\\\\"You need 47 customers to break even. You currently have 38.\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Scenario: \\\\\\\"If you raise prices 15%, breakeven drops to 41 customers\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### Hiring ROI Calculator:\\\\n- \\\\\\\"If you hire a salesperson at $65K + $20K commission potential...\\\\\\\"\\\\n- \\\\\\\"They need to generate $127K in revenue to be ROI-positive\\\\\\\"\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Based on your current close rate of 22%, they need 48 leads/month\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Connected to diagnostic: \\\\\\\"Your Marketing score suggests lead generation is a gap. Fix that first.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### Pricing Optimization Modeler:\\\\n- Current pricing \\\\u2192 revenue at current volume\\\\n- \\\\\\\"What if you raised prices 10%?\\\\\\\" \\\\u2192 projected revenue assuming X% volume loss\\\\n- Industry benchmarks for price sensitivity\\\\n- Connected to Marketing & Sales diagnostic: \\\\\\\"Your value proposition score is 2/5 \\\\u2014 strengthen that before raising prices\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### Revenue Scenario Planner:\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Model: What does $500K revenue look like?\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Sliders: Price per unit, units per month, # customers, retention rate\\\\n- Output: \\\\\\\"To hit $500K, you need 42 customers paying $1,000/mo with 85% annual retention\\\\\\\"\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Your current trajectory puts you at $500K in 18 months\\\\\\\" (or identifies the gap)\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 14. LIFECYCLE REVENUE FEATURES\\\\n\\\\nNot just getting coaches and clients on \\\\u2014 maximizing revenue per relationship over time:\\\\n\\\\n### Automated Re-Engagement:\\\\n- Client goes inactive \\\\u2192 30 days later: \\\\\\\"It's been a month. Your Business Health Score was 67 \\\\u2014 want to check in?\\\\\\\"\\\\n- 90 days: \\\\\\\"3 months since your last session. See how your business has evolved.\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Annual: \\\\\\\"It's your coaching anniversary! Here's your year-in-review.\\\\\\\" (auto-generated from data)\\\\n\\\\n### Upsell Paths (Built Into the Product):\\\\n| Current State | Trigger | Upsell |\\\\n|--------------|---------|--------|\\\\n| 1:1 coaching only | Client mentions team issues | \\\\\\\"Consider adding Team Health Pulse survey\\\\\\\" |\\\\n| No 360\\\\u00b0 feedback | Client's blind spots visible in notes | \\\\\\\"360\\\\u00b0 feedback could validate this\\\\\\\" |\\\\n| Standard engagement | High BHS improvement | \\\\\\\"Your progress is exceptional. Consider a Quarterly Business Review package\\\\\\\" |\\\\n| Individual client | Client is CEO of 50+ person company | \\\\\\\"Your leadership team could benefit from group diagnostics\\\\\\\" |\\\\n| Coaching only | Client mentions board meeting | \\\\\\\"BoldEdge can generate a Board Prep Kit\\\\\\\" |\\\\n\\\\n### Package Bundling:\\\\n- Coach creates custom bundles: \\\\\\\"Growth Accelerator\\\\\\\" = 12 sessions + 2 diagnostics + 1 retreat + monthly KPI review\\\\n- Platform tracks usage against package\\\\n- Auto-alerts: \\\\\\\"Client has 2 sessions remaining in their package. Recommend bundle price: $6,500-$7,500.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### Seasonal Campaigns:\\\\n- **January:** \\\\\\\"New Year Business Health Check\\\\\\\" \\\\u2014 free diagnostic promotion for coaches to offer prospects\\\\n- **Q1:** \\\\\\\"Q1 Planning Workshop\\\\\\\" \\\\u2014 coaches get a turnkey workshop kit (slides, workbook, diagnostic link)\\\\n- **Mid-Year:** \\\\\\\"Mid-Year Business Review\\\\\\\" \\\\u2014 re-diagnostic push for all active clients\\\\n- **Q4:** \\\\\\\"Year in Review\\\\\\\" \\\\u2014 auto-generated annual summary for every client\\\\n- Each campaign comes with: email templates, social media copy, workshop materials, diagnostic links\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 15. ONBOARDING UX \\\\\\\\u2014 THE FIRST 5 MINUTES\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nThe #1 reason SaaS products fail is onboarding. 40-60% of free trial users never come back after day 1. BoldEdge needs the best onboarding in coaching SaaS.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Coach Onboarding Flow (First Login)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Screen 1: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"What brings you here?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"**\\\\\\\\n- [ ] I'm an executive/business coach\\\\\\\\n- [ ] I run peer groups or masterminds\\\\\\\\n- [ ] I'm exploring coaching tools\\\\\\\\n- [ ] My colleague referred me\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\u2192 Routes to personalized setup based on answer\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Screen 2: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Let's set up your practice\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" (60-second form)**\\\\\\\\n- Your name + photo\\\\\\\\n- Coaching specialty (executive, business, leadership, team, career)\\\\\\\\n- Number of active clients (0-5, 6-15, 16-30, 31+)\\\\\\\\n- Primary service (1:1, group, peer advisory, programs, mix)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\u2192 Platform pre-configures dashboard, templates, and workflows based on answers\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Screen 3: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Add your first client\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" (the activation moment)**\\\\\\\\n- Simple form: Client name, email, company name\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Send them a diagnostic?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" toggle (default ON)\\\\\\\\n- Client receives branded email invitation\\\\\\\\n- Coach sees: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Diagnostic sent! You'll see results on your dashboard when they complete it.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\u2192 **This is the \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"aha moment.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"** Coach sees the system working in real-time.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Screen 4: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"While you wait, take your own diagnostic\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"**\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"See how BoldEdge works from the client's perspective\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Coach takes the Business Health Check on their own coaching business\\\\\\\\n- Results appear in a demo dashboard\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"This is what your client will see. Now imagine 10 clients, all tracked here.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Screen 5: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"You're live. Here's your next 3 steps.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"**\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\u2705 Client invited (done)\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\u2b1c Schedule your first session (calendar link or manual)\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\u2b1c Customize your dashboard (pick which KPIs to track)\\\\\\\\n- Progress bar: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"You're 40% set up\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Client Onboarding Flow (First Login)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Step 1:** Branded email from coach: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Welcome to BoldEdge \\\\\\\\u2014 [Coach Name] has invited you\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n**Step 2:** Click \\\\\\\\u2192 lands on diagnostic (no login required for first diagnostic)\\\\\\\\n**Step 3:** Complete diagnostic (48 questions, ~10 minutes, progress bar, save & resume)\\\\\\\\n**Step 4:** Instant results page: Business Health Score + radar chart + top 3 recommendations\\\\\\\\n**Step 5:** \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Create your account to save results and access your dashboard\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n**Step 6:** Client dashboard with pre-populated data from diagnostic\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Key principle:** Client gets VALUE before they create an account. The diagnostic is the product demo.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 3. FREE TIER / FREEMIUM STRATEGY\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### What's Free Forever:\\\\\\\\n- 1 client\\\\\\\\n- Basic diagnostic (no benchmarking)\\\\\\\\n- 3 session notes\\\\\\\\n- Business Health Score\\\\\\\\n- 5 templates\\\\\\\\n- BoldEdge branding on all exports (\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Powered by BoldEdge\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\")\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### What This Achieves:\\\\\\\\n- Every coach can test the core loop (invite client \\\\\\\\u2192 run diagnostic \\\\\\\\u2192 see dashboard) with zero risk\\\\\\\\n- Free users generate diagnostic data that feeds the benchmark network\\\\\\\\n- Free users see \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Unlock benchmarking\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" / \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Add more clients\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" upgrade prompts at natural friction points\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Powered by BoldEdge\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" on free-tier exports = casual contact viral loop\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Upgrade Triggers (natural, not annoying):\\\\\\\\n- Try to add 2nd client \\\\\\\\u2192 \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Upgrade to Starter for up to 10 clients\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Try to export without branding \\\\\\\\u2192 \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Remove BoldEdge branding on Professional\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Try to access 360\\\\\\\\u00b0 feedback \\\\\\\\u2192 \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Available on Professional and above\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Complete 3 months \\\\\\\\u2192 \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"You've coached 1 client for 3 months. See your coaching effectiveness score \\\\\\\\u2014 available on Professional.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 4. MOBILE EXPERIENCE\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Coach Mobile App (Priorities):\\\\\\\\n1. **Session prep brief** \\\\\\\\u2014 swipe through client summary before a session (even in the elevator)\\\\\\\\n2. **Quick note capture** \\\\\\\\u2014 voice-to-text session notes, auto-tagged to client\\\\\\\\n3. **Task quick-add** \\\\\\\\u2014 assign a task to a client in 3 taps\\\\\\\\n4. **Notification center** \\\\\\\\u2014 overdue tasks, upcoming sessions, at-risk alerts\\\\\\\\n5. **Client quick-view** \\\\\\\\u2014 tap client name \\\\\\\\u2192 BHS score, last session, open tasks\\\\\\\\n6. **Offline mode** \\\\\\\\u2014 session notes save locally, sync when connected\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Client Mobile App (Priorities):\\\\\\\\n1. **Business Health Score** \\\\\\\\u2014 big number, front and center, swipe for trend\\\\\\\\n2. **Weekly pulse check-in** \\\\\\\\u2014 push notification Monday 8 AM, complete in 30 seconds\\\\\\\\n3. **Task list** \\\\\\\\u2014 check off completed tasks, see what's due\\\\\\\\n4. **Session reminder** \\\\\\\\u2014 with pre-session self-assessment inline\\\\\\\\n5. **Win journal** \\\\\\\\u2014 quick capture, photo attachment, voice note\\\\\\\\n6. **Habit tracker** \\\\\\\\u2014 daily check-in with streak counter\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Mobile-Specific Features:\\\\\\\\n- **Apple Watch complication** \\\\\\\\u2014 Business Health Score on watch face\\\\\\\\n- **Widget** \\\\\\\\u2014 iOS/Android home screen widget showing BHS + next session\\\\\\\\n- **Push notifications** \\\\\\\\u2014 smart timing based on user activity patterns\\\\\\\\n- **Siri/Google shortcut** \\\\\\\\u2014 \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Hey Siri, add a win to BoldEdge\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\u2192 voice capture\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 6. TRUST, COMPLIANCE & CREDIBILITY SIGNALS\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### ICF Alignment:\\\\\\\\n- Diagnostic questions aligned with ICF Core Competencies\\\\\\\\n- ROI calculation follows ICF-recommended methodology\\\\\\\\n- Platform displays: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"BoldEdge follows ICF measurement guidelines\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Future: Apply for ICF Continuing Coach Education (CCE) credits for BoldEdge training\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Security & Privacy:\\\\\\\\n- SOC 2 Type II compliance roadmap (not needed at launch, but signal intent)\\\\\\\\n- End-to-end encryption for session notes and client data\\\\\\\\n- Client data owned by coach \\\\\\\\u2014 if they leave, they export everything\\\\\\\\n- Data processing agreement (DPA) available for enterprise\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your data is never shared. Period.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" prominently on settings page\\\\\\\\n- Two-factor authentication\\\\\\\\n- Session recording stored encrypted with auto-deletion options\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Trust Signals on Every Page:\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Last backup: 2 minutes ago\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" in footer\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Data encrypted at rest and in transit\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" in security settings\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Served from [region] \\\\\\\\u2014 GDPR compliant\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" for international coaches\\\\\\\\n- Client-facing: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your responses are confidential and shared only with your coach\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Professional Credibility:\\\\\\\\n- Coach profile: display certifications (ICF ACC/PCC/MCC, DISC, EQ-i, StrengthsFinder)\\\\\\\\n- Verified badge: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"BoldEdge Verified Coach\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" (completed training + has active clients)\\\\\\\\n- Testimonial display: verified outcome badges from the platform\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Member since [date]\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" on profile\\\\\\\\n- Aggregate stats: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Has coached 23 clients on BoldEdge with average 19-point BHS improvement\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 7. API & ECOSYSTEM PLAY\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Public API (Business+ tier):\\\\\\\\n- RESTful API with full documentation\\\\\\\\n- Endpoints: clients, diagnostics, sessions, tasks, KPIs, reports\\\\\\\\n- Webhook events: diagnostic_completed, session_completed, task_completed, score_changed\\\\\\\\n- Use cases:\\\\\\\\n  - Custom dashboards in client's existing tools\\\\\\\\n  - Automated data pulls into BI platforms (Tableau, Looker, PowerBI)\\\\\\\\n  - CRM sync (push BoldEdge data to Salesforce/HubSpot)\\\\\\\\n  - Custom integrations coaches build themselves\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Embeddable Widgets:\\\\\\\\n- **Diagnostic embed** \\\\\\\\u2014 coach puts diagnostic on their own website. Client completes it \\\\\\\\u2192 data flows to BoldEdge.\\\\\\\\n  - `<iframe src=\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"boldedge.io/embed/diagnostic/[coach-id]\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" />`\\\\\\\\n  - Branded with coach's logo and colors\\\\\\\\n  - This is a massive lead-gen tool. Every coach website becomes a BoldEdge acquisition channel.\\\\\\\\n- **Business Health Score badge** \\\\\\\\u2014 client embeds their score on their own site\\\\\\\\n  - Like a \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"BBB Accredited\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" badge but for business health\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Business Health Score: 78 | Verified by BoldEdge\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  - Click \\\\\\\\u2192 public profile with score trend (if client opts in)\\\\\\\\n  - Viral loop: other businesses see it and want their own score\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Zapier/Make Integration:\\\\\\\\n- Triggers: New client, Diagnostic completed, Session completed, Task overdue, Score changed, Recommendation accepted\\\\\\\\n- Actions: Create client, Add KPI data point, Create task, Send notification\\\\\\\\n- Use cases:\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"When diagnostic is completed \\\\\\\\u2192 send Slack notification to coach\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"When task is overdue by 3 days \\\\\\\\u2192 send reminder via Twilio SMS\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"When new client added \\\\\\\\u2192 create Google Drive folder\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"When session completed \\\\\\\\u2192 log to Airtable tracking sheet\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 8. ADVANCED GAMIFICATION\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nBeyond badges \\\\\\\\u2014 gamification that drives real behavioral change:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### For Clients:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Levels System:**\\\\\\\\n- Level 1: Newcomer (completed intake)\\\\\\\\n- Level 2: Committed (completed first diagnostic)\\\\\\\\n- Level 3: In Motion (completed 3 sessions + 5 tasks)\\\\\\\\n- Level 4: Gaining Traction (BHS improved 10+ points)\\\\\\\\n- Level 5: Transforming (BHS improved 20+ points)\\\\\\\\n- Level 6: High Performer (BHS above 75)\\\\\\\\n- Level 7: Elite (BHS above 85 for 3+ months)\\\\\\\\n- Level 8: Champion (BHS above 90 + refers another client)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nEach level unlocks:\\\\\\\\n- A visual badge on their dashboard\\\\\\\\n- A shareable achievement graphic\\\\\\\\n- Access to progressively advanced resources\\\\\\\\n- At Level 5+: invitation to join a peer group of similar-level clients\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Streaks:**\\\\\\\\n- Weekly pulse check-in streak: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"12 weeks in a row! \\\\\\\\ud83d\\\\\\\\udd25\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Task completion streak: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Perfect month \\\\\\\\u2014 all tasks completed on time!\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Session attendance streak: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"6 consecutive sessions attended\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Broken streaks trigger gentle re-engagement: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"You missed your check-in this week. Take 30 seconds to get back on track.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Challenges:**\\\\\\\\n- Coach assigns a 30-day challenge: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Delegate one decision per day for 30 days\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Client checks in daily\\\\\\\\n- Completion = badge + highlight in next session prep\\\\\\\\n- Peer group challenges: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Which group member can improve their Financial score the most in 90 days?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### For Coaches:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Coaching Milestones:**\\\\\\\\n- 10th client onboarded\\\\\\\\n- 100th session completed\\\\\\\\n- 50th diagnostic run\\\\\\\\n- First client reaches BHS 80+\\\\\\\\n- 5 clients with positive ROI verified\\\\\\\\n- 1,000 coaching hours logged\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Leaderboard (opt-in, anonymized):**\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your average client BHS improvement: 18 points (top 25% of BoldEdge coaches)\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your client retention rate: 88% (top 15%)\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your task completion rate: 85% (above average)\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Not competitive \\\\\\\\u2014 more like \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"here's where you stand\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" benchmarking\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 9. WHITE-LABEL DEEP MECHANICS\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nFor Enterprise tier ($499-999/mo) \\\\\\\\u2014 what makes white-label actually valuable:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Visual Customization:\\\\\\\\n- Custom logo (replaces BoldEdge logo everywhere)\\\\\\\\n- Custom color scheme (primary, secondary, accent)\\\\\\\\n- Custom fonts\\\\\\\\n- Custom email templates (header, footer, colors)\\\\\\\\n- Custom domain: `coaching.mereditheicher.com`\\\\\\\\n- Custom favicon\\\\\\\\n- Removable \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Powered by BoldEdge\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" footer\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Content Customization:\\\\\\\\n- Custom diagnostic questions (add, remove, reword)\\\\\\\\n- Custom scoring weights\\\\\\\\n- Custom recommendation library\\\\\\\\n- Custom templates (replace defaults with coach's own)\\\\\\\\n- Custom onboarding flow text\\\\\\\\n- Custom email copy\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Branding Deliverables:\\\\\\\\n- All PDF exports use coach's branding\\\\\\\\n- Client portal shows coach's branding only\\\\\\\\n- Proposals and agreements branded\\\\\\\\n- Diagnostic emails branded\\\\\\\\n- ROI reports branded\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### What BoldEdge Still Powers (Under the Hood):\\\\\\\\n- All infrastructure and hosting\\\\\\\\n- AI intelligence and benchmarking\\\\\\\\n- Workflow automation engine\\\\\\\\n- Data storage and security\\\\\\\\n- Platform updates and new features\\\\\\\\n- Support and maintenance\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Pitch to coaches:** \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your brand. Our brain. Give your clients a world-class technology experience with your name on it.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 10. COACH SUCCESS SYSTEM (Reduce Coach Churn)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nThe biggest risk to BoldEdge is coach churn. If coaches leave, everything stops. Build systems to make coaches successful:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Coach Onboarding Checklist (First 30 Days):\\\\\\\\n- [ ] Week 1: Complete profile, take personal diagnostic, add first client\\\\\\\\n- [ ] Week 2: Client completes diagnostic, coach reviews results, first session prep used\\\\\\\\n- [ ] Week 3: First post-session follow-up workflow runs, 2+ tasks assigned\\\\\\\\n- [ ] Week 4: Second client added OR second session with first client\\\\\\\\n- Progress tracked on \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Coach Success Dashboard\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" (internal admin view)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Automated Coach Health Monitoring:\\\\\\\\n- **Green:** Coach has 3+ active clients, runs sessions weekly, uses diagnostic\\\\\\\\n- **Yellow:** Coach has 1-2 clients, sessions sporadic, some features unused\\\\\\\\n- **Red:** Coach hasn't logged in 14+ days, no sessions in 30 days\\\\\\\\n- Automated nudges for Yellow/Red:\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"You haven't run a diagnostic in 3 weeks. Want to send one to [client name]?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Most BoldEdge coaches see retention improve after using session prep briefs. Try it before your next session.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Coach Community:\\\\\\\\n- Private forum/Slack for BoldEdge coaches\\\\\\\\n- Monthly webinar: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"BoldEdge Best Practices\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" featuring a power user\\\\\\\\n- Template sharing: coaches contribute templates to a shared library\\\\\\\\n- Peer matching: connect coaches in non-competing markets for knowledge exchange\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Coach of the Month\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" spotlight\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Coach Training Resources:\\\\\\\\n- Video library: How to use every feature (2-3 min each)\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"The BoldEdge Method\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\u2014 a coaching methodology built around the diagnostic\\\\\\\\n  - Step 1: Diagnostic \\\\\\\\u2192 Baseline\\\\\\\\n  - Step 2: Priority Gap Identification \\\\\\\\u2192 Focus Areas\\\\\\\\n  - Step 3: Coaching Plan \\\\\\\\u2192 90-Day Objectives\\\\\\\\n  - Step 4: Session Cadence \\\\\\\\u2192 Weekly/Bi-weekly\\\\\\\\n  - Step 5: Task & Action Loop \\\\\\\\u2192 Between-Session Accountability\\\\\\\\n  - Step 6: Re-Diagnostic \\\\\\\\u2192 Measure Progress\\\\\\\\n  - Step 7: ROI Report \\\\\\\\u2192 Prove Value\\\\\\\\n  - Step 8: Renewal \\\\\\\\u2192 Continue Engagement\\\\\\\\n- Certification program (as described in Doc 06)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 11. EVENT & OFFLINE INTEGRATION\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nCoaching doesn't only happen online. BoldEdge should bridge digital and in-person:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Conference/Workshop Mode:\\\\\\\\n- Coach runs a workshop at a conference\\\\\\\\n- Audience scans QR code \\\\\\\\u2192 takes mini-diagnostic (10 questions) on phone\\\\\\\\n- Instant results on their phone\\\\\\\\n- Coach shows aggregate audience results on screen: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"This room's biggest gap is Strategy\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- CTA: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Want the full diagnostic? Connect with [coach name]\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Every attendee becomes a lead in BoldEdge CRM\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Retreat Integration (expanded from Doc 06):\\\\\\\\n- **Pre-retreat survey** \\\\\\\\u2014 team members complete individual diagnostics\\\\\\\\n- **Live session tools:**\\\\\\\\n  - Polling: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"What's your team's #1 challenge?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" (live results on screen)\\\\\\\\n  - Breakout group assignment based on diagnostic profiles\\\\\\\\n  - Commitment capture: each person enters their 90-day commitment on phone\\\\\\\\n- **Post-retreat:**\\\\\\\\n  - All commitments tracked as tasks in BoldEdge\\\\\\\\n  - 30/60/90 day automated check-ins\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Retreat Impact Report\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" generated at 90 days\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Speaking Engagement Lead Capture:\\\\\\\\n- Coach gives a talk \\\\\\\\u2192 references Business Health Score\\\\\\\\n- Audience texts keyword to number \\\\\\\\u2192 gets diagnostic link\\\\\\\\n- Or scans QR \\\\\\\\u2192 same flow\\\\\\\\n- All leads tracked in BoldEdge with source tag: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Event: [Name] [Date]\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 12. AI CAPABILITIES \\\\\\\\u2014 NEXT LEVEL\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nBeyond what's in Docs 05-07:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Voice Notes \\\\\\\\u2192 Structured Data:\\\\\\\\n- Coach records voice note after session on phone\\\\\\\\n- AI transcribes \\\\\\\\u2192 extracts: key themes, action items, mood assessment, follow-up questions\\\\\\\\n- Auto-files to correct client's session record\\\\\\\\n- Coach reviews and approves \\\\\\\\u2192 action items auto-created as tasks\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### AI Coaching Debrief Partner:\\\\\\\\n- After a session, coach can \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"debrief\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" with AI:\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"The session with James went off track. He kept avoiding the financial review discussion.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  - AI responds: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"This is the 3rd time James has avoided financials. His Financial diagnostic score is 38. Consider opening next session with: 'James, I've noticed a pattern...'\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Not replacing coaching supervision \\\\\\\\u2014 augmenting it with data-backed reflection\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Predictive Client Matching:\\\\\\\\n- When a new prospect enters (via diagnostic or referral), AI suggests:\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"This prospect profiles similarly to your most successful clients (Sarah, Mark). Recommend 1:1 Premium engagement.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"This prospect's profile matches clients who churn within 3 months at the Starter level. Consider a deeper onboarding.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Natural Language KPI Entry:\\\\\\\\n- Client types: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Revenue was $142K this month, up from $128K last month. Hired 2 new people.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- AI parses \\\\\\\\u2192 updates KPIs: Revenue: $142,000 (+10.9%), Headcount: +2\\\\\\\\n- Coach sees updated dashboard without manual data entry\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### AI-Generated Quarterly Business Review:\\\\\\\\n- Every 90 days, AI auto-generates a comprehensive review:\\\\\\\\n  - BHS trend\\\\\\\\n  - KPI changes\\\\\\\\n  - Tasks completed and impact\\\\\\\\n  - Diagnostic score changes\\\\\\\\n  - Sessions attended\\\\\\\\n  - Wins logged\\\\\\\\n  - Recommendations accepted and status\\\\\\\\n- Coach reviews, edits, and sends to client as a branded PDF\\\\\\\\n- Saves 2-3 hours per client per quarter\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 13. FINANCIAL MODELING SUITE (For Business-Focused Coaching)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nBeyond the cash runway calculator \\\\\\\\u2014 a light financial modeling layer:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Break-Even Calculator:\\\\\\\\n- Inputs: Fixed costs, variable costs, average revenue per unit\\\\\\\\n- Output: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"You need 47 customers to break even. You currently have 38.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Scenario: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"If you raise prices 15%, breakeven drops to 41 customers\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Hiring ROI Calculator:\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"If you hire a salesperson at $65K + $20K commission potential...\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"They need to generate $127K in revenue to be ROI-positive\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Based on your current close rate of 22%, they need 48 leads/month\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Connected to diagnostic: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your Marketing score suggests lead generation is a gap. Fix that first.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Pricing Optimization Modeler:\\\\\\\\n- Current pricing \\\\\\\\u2192 revenue at current volume\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"What if you raised prices 10%?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\u2192 projected revenue assuming X% volume loss\\\\\\\\n- Industry benchmarks for price sensitivity\\\\\\\\n- Connected to Marketing & Sales diagnostic: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your value proposition score is 2/5 \\\\\\\\u2014 strengthen that before raising prices\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Revenue Scenario Planner:\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Model: What does $500K revenue look like?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Sliders: Price per unit, units per month, # customers, retention rate\\\\\\\\n- Output: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"To hit $500K, you need 42 customers paying $1,000/mo with 85% annual retention\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your current trajectory puts you at $500K in 18 months\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" (or identifies the gap)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 14. LIFECYCLE REVENUE FEATURES\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nNot just getting coaches and clients on \\\\\\\\u2014 maximizing revenue per relationship over time:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Automated Re-Engagement:\\\\\\\\n- Client goes inactive \\\\\\\\u2192 30 days later: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"It's been a month. Your Business Health Score was 67 \\\\\\\\u2014 want to check in?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- 90 days: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"3 months since your last session. Companies that maintain coaching see 2.4x better outcomes.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- 6 months: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Time for your semi-annual diagnostic? See how your business has evolved.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Annual: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"It's your coaching anniversary! Here's your year-in-review.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" (auto-generated from data)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Upsell Paths (Built Into the Product):\\\\\\\\n| Current State | Trigger | Upsell |\\\\\\\\n|--------------|---------|--------|\\\\\\\\n| 1:1 coaching only | Client mentions team issues | \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Consider adding Team Health Pulse survey\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" |\\\\\\\\n| No 360\\\\\\\\u00b0 feedback | Client's blind spots visible in notes | \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"360\\\\\\\\u00b0 feedback could validate this\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" |\\\\\\\\n| Standard engagement | High BHS improvement | \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your progress is exceptional. Consider a Quarterly Business Review package\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" |\\\\\\\\n| Individual client | Client is CEO of 50+ person company | \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your leadership team could benefit from group diagnostics\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" |\\\\\\\\n| Coaching only | Client mentions board meeting | \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"BoldEdge can generate a Board Prep Kit\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" |\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Package Bundling:\\\\\\\\n- Coach creates custom bundles: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Growth Accelerator\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" = 12 sessions + 2 diagnostics + 1 retreat + monthly KPI review\\\\\\\\n- Platform tracks usage against package\\\\\\\\n- Auto-alerts: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Client has 2 sessions remaining in their package. Renewal conversation recommended.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Suggested pricing based on component value: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"This bundle's component value is $8,500. Recommend bundle price: $6,500-$7,500.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Seasonal Campaigns:\\\\\\\\n- **January:** \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"New Year Business Health Check\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\u2014 free diagnostic promotion for coaches to offer prospects\\\\\\\\n- **Q1:** \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Q1 Planning Workshop\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\u2014 coaches get a turnkey workshop kit (slides, workbook, diagnostic link)\\\\\\\\n- **Mid-Year:** \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Mid-Year Business Review\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\u2014 re-diagnostic push for all active clients\\\\\\\\n- **Q4:** \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Year in Review\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\u2014 auto-generated annual summary for every client\\\\\\\\n- Each campaign comes with: email templates, social media copy, workshop materials, diagnostic links\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 15. ADMIN & PLATFORM INTELLIGENCE (For Ned)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Product Analytics Dashboard:\\\\\\\\n- Feature adoption: which features are used, how often, by whom\\\\\\\\n- Activation funnel: signup \\\\\\\\u2192 first client \\\\\\\\u2192 first diagnostic \\\\\\\\u2192 first session \\\\\\\\u2192 paid conversion\\\\\\\\n- Cohort analysis: which month's signups retained best? Why?\\\\\\\\n- Revenue by tier, by feature, by geography\\\\\\\\n- NPS by segment\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Churn Prediction:\\\\\\\\n- ML model trained on: login frequency, feature usage, client count, session frequency, support tickets\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Coach Jane has a 72% probability of churning in the next 30 days\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Risk factors: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Hasn't logged in 12 days, only 1 active client, never used session prep\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Recommended intervention: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Personal outreach + offer onboarding call\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### A/B Testing Framework:\\\\\\\\n- Test onboarding flows, pricing pages, upgrade prompts, email templates\\\\\\\\n- Track: conversion rate, activation rate, retention at 30/60/90 days\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Onboarding Flow B (with personal diagnostic) has 34% higher activation than Flow A\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Partner Dashboard:\\\\\\\\n- Meredith (and future partners): see their referrals, commissions, client activity\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"This month: 3 referrals, 2 converted, $297 in commissions\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your referral link was clicked 47 times. 12 started trials. 2 converted.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Leaderboard of top partners\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 16. In funnels, once a user reaches the end, the process resets. BoldEdge needs the best onboarding in coaching SaaS.\\\\n\\\\n### Coach Onboarding Flow (First Login)\\\\n\\\\n**Screen 1: \\\\\\\"What brings you here?\\\\\\\"**\\\\n- [ ] I'm an executive/business coach\\\\n- [ ] I run peer groups or masterminds\\\\n- [ ] I'm exploring coaching tools\\\\n- [ ] My colleague referred me\\\\n\\\\u2192 Routes to personalized setup based on answer\\\\n\\\\n**Screen 2: \\\\\\\"Let's set up your practice\\\\\\\" (60-second form)**\\\\n- Your name + photo\\\\n- Coaching specialty (executive, business, leadership, team, career)\\\\n- Number of active clients (0-5, 6-15, 16-30, 31+)\\\\n- Primary service (1:1, group, peer advisory, programs, mix)\\\\n\\\\u2192 Platform pre-configures dashboard, templates, and workflows based on answers\\\\n\\\\n**Screen 3: \\\\\\\"Add your first client\\\\\\\" (the activation moment)**\\\\n- Simple form: Client name, email, company name\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Send them a diagnostic?\\\\\\\" toggle (default ON)\\\\n- Client receives branded email invitation\\\\n- Coach sees: \\\\\\\"Diagnostic sent! You'll see results on your dashboard when they complete it.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\u2192 **This is the \\\\\\\"aha moment.\\\\\\\"** Coach sees the system working in real-time.\\\\n\\\\n**Screen 4: \\\\\\\"While you wait, take your own diagnostic\\\\\\\"**\\\\n- \\\\\\\"See how BoldEdge works from the client's perspective\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Coach takes the Business Health Check on their own coaching business\\\\n- Results appear in a demo dashboard\\\\n- \\\\\\\"This is what your client will see. Now imagine 10 clients, all tracked here.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n**Screen 5: \\\\\\\"You're live. Here's your next 3 steps.\\\\\\\"**\\\\n- \\\\u2705 Client invited (done)\\\\n- \\\\u2b1c Schedule your first session (calendar link or manual)\\\\n- \\\\u2b1c Customize your dashboard (pick which KPIs to track)\\\\n- Progress bar: \\\\\\\"You're 40% set up\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### Client Onboarding Flow (First Login)\\\\n\\\\n**Step 1:** Branded email from coach: \\\\\\\"Welcome to BoldEdge \\\\u2014 [Coach Name] has invited you\\\\\\\"\\\\n**Step 2:** Click \\\\u2192 lands on diagnostic (no login required for first diagnostic)\\\\n**Step 3:** Complete diagnostic (48 questions, ~10 minutes, progress bar, save & resume)\\\\n**Step 4:** Instant results page: Business Health Score + radar chart + top 3 recommendations\\\\n**Step 5:** \\\\\\\"Create your account to save results and access your dashboard\\\\\\\"\\\\n**Step 6:** Client dashboard with pre-populated data from diagnostic\\\\n\\\\n**Key principle:** Client gets VALUE before they create an account. The diagnostic is the product demo.\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 3. FREE TIER / FREEMIUM STRATEGY\\\\n\\\\n### What's Free Forever:\\\\n- 1 client\\\\n- Basic diagnostic (no benchmarking)\\\\n- 3 session notes\\\\n- Business Health Score\\\\n- 5 templates\\\\n- BoldEdge branding on all exports (\\\\\\\"Powered by BoldEdge\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n### What This Achieves:\\\\n- Every coach can test the core loop (invite client \\\\u2192 run diagnostic \\\\u2192 see dashboard) with zero risk\\\\n- Free users generate diagnostic data that feeds the benchmark network\\\\n- Free users see \\\\\\\"Unlock benchmarking\\\\\\\" / \\\\\\\"Add more clients\\\\\\\" upgrade prompts at natural friction points\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Powered by BoldEdge\\\\\\\" on free-tier exports = casual contact viral loop\\\\n\\\\n### Upgrade Triggers (natural, not annoying):\\\\n- Try to add 2nd client \\\\u2192 \\\\\\\"Upgrade to Starter for up to 10 clients\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Try to export without branding \\\\u2192 \\\\\\\"Remove BoldEdge branding on Professional\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Try to access 360\\\\u00b0 feedback \\\\u2192 \\\\\\\"Available on Professional and above\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Complete 3 months \\\\u2192 \\\\\\\"You've coached 1 client for 3 months. See your coaching effectiveness score \\\\u2014 available on Professional.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 4. CONTENT ENGINE (Built Into the Product)\\\\n\\\\n### SEO Content That Writes Itself:\\\\nBoldEdge's data generates content automatically:\\\\n\\\\n| Auto-Generated Content | SEO Target | Frequency |\\\\n|----------------------|-----------|-----------|\\\\n| \\\\\\\"Average Business Health Score by Industry\\\\\\\" | \\\\\\\"business health benchmark [industry]\\\\\\\" | Quarterly |\\\\n| \\\\\\\"Top 5 Gaps in [State] Small Businesses\\\\\\\" | \\\\\\\"small business challenges [state]\\\\\\\" | Quarterly |\\\\n| \\\\\\\"What the Best-Run Small Businesses Do Differently\\\\\\\" | \\\\\\\"how to improve business operations\\\\\\\" | Annual |\\\\n| \\\\\\\"Coaching ROI: Real Numbers from 500+ Engagements\\\\\\\" | \\\\\\\"executive coaching ROI\\\\\\\" | Annual |\\\\n| Template library pages | \\\\\\\"executive coaching session template\\\\\\\" | Ongoing |\\\\n| Framework guides | \\\\\\\"GROW model coaching questions\\\\\\\" | Evergreen |\\\\n\\\\n### Coach-Generated Content (UGC Loop):\\\\n- Coaches can publish anonymized case studies from their BoldEdge data\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Sarah improved her client's Operations score by 28 points in 6 months. Here's how.\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Auto-generated from platform data (with coach approval)\\\\n- Published on BoldEdge blog + coach's profile\\\\n- SEO + social proof + lead generation for the coach\\\\n\\\\n### Knowledge Base Within the Product:\\\\nNot a separate wiki \\\\u2014 embedded contextually:\\\\n- Mouse over \\\\\\\"Operations\\\\\\\" on any diagnostic \\\\u2192 tooltip: \\\\\\\"Operations measures your business processes, technology, delivery quality, and efficiency. Companies scoring 75+ grow 30% faster.\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Click any recommendation \\\\u2192 \\\\\\\"Why this matters\\\\\\\" section with data\\\\n- Every framework referenced includes a \\\\\\\"Learn more\\\\\\\" expandable with 2-3 paragraphs + source links\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Coaching Corner\\\\\\\" section: weekly article curated for coaches (best practices, industry trends, technique spotlights)\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 5. MOBILE EXPERIENCE\\\\n\\\\n### Coach Mobile App (Priorities):\\\\n1. **Session prep brief** \\\\u2014 swipe through client summary before a session (even in the elevator)\\\\n2. **Quick note capture** \\\\u2014 voice-to-text session notes, auto-tagged to client\\\\n3. **Task quick-add** \\\\u2014 assign a task to a client in 3 taps\\\\n4. **Notification center** \\\\u2014 overdue tasks, upcoming sessions, at-risk alerts\\\\n5. **Client quick-view** \\\\u2014 tap client name \\\\u2192 BHS score, last session, open tasks\\\\n6. **Offline mode** \\\\u2014 session notes save locally, sync when connected\\\\n\\\\n### Client Mobile App (Priorities):\\\\n1. **Business Health Score** \\\\u2014 big number, front and center, swipe for trend\\\\n2. **Weekly pulse check-in** \\\\u2014 push notification Monday 8 AM, complete in 30 seconds\\\\n3. **Task list** \\\\u2014 check off completed tasks, see what's due\\\\n4. **Session reminder** \\\\u2014 with pre-session self-assessment inline\\\\n5. **Win journal** \\\\u2014 quick capture, photo attachment, voice note\\\\n6. **Habit tracker** \\\\u2014 daily check-in with streak counter\\\\n\\\\n### Mobile-Specific Features:\\\\n- **Apple Watch complication** \\\\u2014 Business Health Score on watch face\\\\n- **Widget** \\\\u2014 iOS/Android home screen widget showing BHS + next session\\\\n- **Push notifications** \\\\u2014 smart timing based on user activity patterns\\\\n- **Siri/Google shortcut** \\\\u2014 \\\\\\\"Hey Siri, add a win to BoldEdge\\\\\\\" \\\\u2192 voice capture\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 6. TRUST, COMPLIANCE & CREDIBILITY SIGNALS\\\\n\\\\n### ICF Alignment:\\\\n- Diagnostic questions aligned with ICF Core Competencies\\\\n- ROI calculation follows ICF-recommended methodology\\\\n- Platform displays: \\\\\\\"BoldEdge follows ICF measurement guidelines\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Future: Apply for ICF Continuing Coach Education (CCE) credits for BoldEdge training\\\\n\\\\n### Security & Privacy:\\\\n- SOC 2 Type II compliance roadmap (not needed at launch, but signal intent)\\\\n- End-to-end encryption for session notes and client data\\\\n- Client data owned by coach \\\\u2014 if they leave, they export everything\\\\n- Data processing agreement (DPA) available for enterprise\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Your data is never shared. Period.\\\\\\\" prominently on settings page\\\\n- Two-factor authentication\\\\n- Session recording stored encrypted with auto-deletion options\\\\n\\\\n### Trust Signals on Every Page:\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Last backup: 2 minutes ago\\\\\\\" in footer\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Data encrypted at rest and in transit\\\\\\\" in security settings\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Served from [region] \\\\u2014 GDPR compliant\\\\\\\" for international coaches\\\\n- Client-facing: \\\\\\\"Your responses are confidential and shared only with your coach\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### Professional Credibility:\\\\n- Coach profile: display certifications (ICF ACC/PCC/MCC, DISC, EQ-i, StrengthsFinder)\\\\n- Verified badge: \\\\\\\"BoldEdge Verified Coach\\\\\\\" (completed training + has active clients)\\\\n- Testimonial display: verified outcome badges from the platform\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Member since [date]\\\\\\\" on profile\\\\n- Aggregate stats: \\\\\\\"Has coached 23 clients on BoldEdge with average 19-point BHS improvement\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 7. API & ECOSYSTEM PLAY\\\\n\\\\n### Public API (Business+ tier):\\\\n- RESTful API with full documentation\\\\n- Endpoints: clients, diagnostics, sessions, tasks, KPIs, reports\\\\n- Webhook events: diagnostic_completed, session_completed, task_completed, score_changed\\\\n- Use cases:\\\\n  - Custom dashboards in client's existing tools\\\\n  - Automated data pulls into BI platforms (Tableau, Looker, PowerBI)\\\\n  - CRM sync (push BoldEdge data to Salesforce/HubSpot)\\\\n  - Custom integrations coaches build themselves\\\\n\\\\n### Embeddable Widgets:\\\\n- **Diagnostic embed** \\\\u2014 coach puts diagnostic on their own website. Client completes it \\\\u2192 data flows to BoldEdge.\\\\n  - `<iframe src=\\\\\\\"boldedge.io/embed/diagnostic/[coach-id]\\\\\\\" />`\\\\n  - Branded with coach's logo and colors\\\\n  - This is a massive lead-gen tool. Every coach website becomes a BoldEdge acquisition channel.\\\\n- **Business Health Score badge** \\\\u2014 client embeds their score on their own site\\\\n  - Like a \\\\\\\"BBB Accredited\\\\\\\" badge but for business health\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"Business Health Score: 78 | Verified by BoldEdge\\\\\\\"\\\\n  - Click \\\\u2192 public profile with score trend (if client opts in)\\\\n  - Viral loop: other businesses see it and want their own score\\\\n\\\\n### Zapier/Make Integration:\\\\n- Triggers: New client, Diagnostic completed, Session completed, Task overdue, Score changed, Recommendation accepted\\\\n- Actions: Create client, Add KPI data point, Create task, Send notification\\\\n- Use cases:\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"When diagnostic is completed \\\\u2192 send Slack notification to coach\\\\\\\"\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"When task is overdue by 3 days \\\\u2192 send reminder via Twilio SMS\\\\\\\"\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"When new client added \\\\u2192 create Google Drive folder\\\\\\\"\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"When session completed \\\\u2192 log to Airtable tracking sheet\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 8. ADVANCED GAMIFICATION\\\\n\\\\nBeyond badges \\\\u2014 gamification that drives real behavioral change:\\\\n\\\\n### For Clients:\\\\n\\\\n**Levels System:**\\\\n- Level 1: Newcomer (completed intake)\\\\n- Level 2: Committed (completed first diagnostic)\\\\n- Level 3: In Motion (completed 3 sessions + 5 tasks)\\\\n- Level 4: Gaining Traction (BHS improved 10+ points)\\\\n- Level 5: Transforming (BHS improved 20+ points)\\\\n- Level 6: High Performer (BHS above 75)\\\\n- Level 7: Elite (BHS above 85 for 3+ months)\\\\n- Level 8: Champion (BHS above 90 + refers another client)\\\\n\\\\nEach level unlocks:\\\\n- A visual badge on their dashboard\\\\n- A shareable achievement graphic\\\\n- Access to progressively advanced resources\\\\n- At Level 5+: invitation to join a peer group of similar-level clients\\\\n\\\\n**Streaks:**\\\\n- Weekly pulse check-in streak: \\\\\\\"12 weeks in a row! \\\\ud83d\\\\udd25\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Task completion streak: \\\\\\\"Perfect month \\\\u2014 all tasks completed on time!\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Session attendance streak: \\\\\\\"6 consecutive sessions attended\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Broken streaks trigger gentle re-engagement: \\\\\\\"You missed your check-in this week. Take 30 seconds to get back on track.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n**Challenges:**\\\\n- Coach assigns a 30-day challenge: \\\\\\\"Delegate one decision per day for 30 days\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Client checks in daily\\\\n- Completion = badge + highlight in next session prep\\\\n- Peer group challenges: \\\\\\\"Which group member can improve their Financial score the most in 90 days?\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### For Coaches:\\\\n\\\\n**Coaching Milestones:**\\\\n- 10th client onboarded\\\\n- 100th session completed\\\\n- 50th diagnostic run\\\\n- First client reaches BHS 80+\\\\n- 5 clients with positive ROI verified\\\\n- 1,000 coaching hours logged\\\\n\\\\n**Leaderboard (opt-in, anonymized):**\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Your average client BHS improvement: 18 points (top 25% of BoldEdge coaches)\\\\\\\"\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Your client retention rate: 88% (top 15%)\\\\\\\"\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Your task completion rate: 85% (above average)\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Not competitive \\\\u2014 more like \\\\\\\"here's where you stand\\\\\\\" benchmarking\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 9. WHITE-LABEL DEEP MECHANICS\\\\n\\\\nFor Enterprise tier ($499-999/mo) \\\\u2014 what makes white-label actually valuable:\\\\n\\\\n### Visual Customization:\\\\n- Custom logo (replaces BoldEdge logo everywhere)\\\\n- Custom color scheme (primary, secondary, accent)\\\\n- Custom fonts\\\\n- Custom email templates (header, footer, colors)\\\\n- Custom domain: `coaching.mereditheicher.com`\\\\n- Custom favicon\\\\n- Removable \\\\\\\"Powered by BoldEdge\\\\\\\" footer\\\\n\\\\n### Content Customization:\\\\n- Custom diagnostic questions (add, remove, reword)\\\\n- Custom scoring weights\\\\n- Custom recommendation library\\\\n- Custom templates (replace defaults with coach's own)\\\\n- Custom onboarding flow text\\\\n- Custom email copy\\\\n\\\\n### Branding Deliverables:\\\\n- All PDF exports use coach's branding\\\\n- Client portal shows coach's branding only\\\\n- Proposals and agreements branded\\\\n- Diagnostic emails branded\\\\n- ROI reports branded\\\\n\\\\n### What BoldEdge Still Powers (Under the Hood):\\\\n- All infrastructure and hosting\\\\n- AI intelligence and benchmarking\\\\n- Workflow automation engine\\\\n- Data storage and security\\\\n- Platform updates and new features\\\\n- Support and maintenance\\\\n\\\\n**Pitch to coaches:** \\\\\\\"Your brand. Our brain. Give your clients a world-class technology experience with your name on it.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 10. COACH SUCCESS SYSTEM (Reduce Coach Churn)\\\\n\\\\nThe biggest risk to BoldEdge is coach churn. If coaches leave, everything stops. Build systems to make coaches successful:\\\\n\\\\n### Coach Onboarding Checklist (First 30 Days):\\\\n- [ ] Week 1: Complete profile, take personal diagnostic, add first client\\\\n- [ ] Week 2: Client completes diagnostic, coach reviews results, first session prep used\\\\n- [ ] Week 3: First post-session follow-up workflow runs, 2+ tasks assigned\\\\n- [ ] Week 4: Second client added OR second session with first client\\\\n- Progress tracked on \\\\\\\"Coach Success Dashboard\\\\\\\" (internal admin view)\\\\n\\\\n### Automated Coach Health Monitoring:\\\\n- **Green:** Coach has 3+ active clients, runs sessions weekly, uses diagnostic\\\\n- **Yellow:** Coach has 1-2 clients, sessions sporadic, some features unused\\\\n- **Red:** Coach hasn't logged in 14+ days, no sessions in 30 days\\\\n- Automated nudges for Yellow/Red:\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"You haven't run a diagnostic in 3 weeks. Want to send one to [client name]?\\\\\\\"\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"Most BoldEdge coaches see retention improve after using session prep briefs. Try it before your next session.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### Coach Community:\\\\n- Private forum/Slack for BoldEdge coaches\\\\n- Monthly webinar: \\\\\\\"BoldEdge Best Practices\\\\\\\" featuring a power user\\\\n- Template sharing: coaches contribute templates to a shared library\\\\n- Peer matching: connect coaches in non-competing markets for knowledge exchange\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Coach of the Month\\\\\\\" spotlight\\\\n\\\\n### Coach Training Resources:\\\\n- Video library: How to use every feature (2-3 min each)\\\\n- \\\\\\\"The BoldEdge Method\\\\\\\" \\\\u2014 a coaching methodology built around the diagnostic\\\\n  - Step 1: Diagnostic \\\\u2192 Baseline\\\\n  - Step 2: Priority Gap Identification \\\\u2192 Focus Areas\\\\n  - Step 3: Coaching Plan \\\\u2192 90-Day Objectives\\\\n  - Step 4: Session Cadence \\\\u2192 Weekly/Bi-weekly\\\\n  - Step 5: Task & Action Loop \\\\u2192 Between-Session Accountability\\\\n  - Step 6: Re-Diagnostic \\\\u2192 Measure Progress\\\\n  - Step 7: ROI Report \\\\u2192 Prove Value\\\\n  - Step 8: Renewal \\\\u2192 Continue Engagement\\\\n- Certification program (as described in Doc 06)\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 11. EVENT & OFFLINE INTEGRATION\\\\n\\\\nCoaching doesn't only happen online. BoldEdge should bridge digital and in-person:\\\\n\\\\n### Conference/Workshop Mode:\\\\n- Coach runs a workshop at a conference\\\\n- Audience scans QR code \\\\u2192 takes mini-diagnostic (10 questions) on phone\\\\n- Instant results on their phone\\\\n- Coach shows aggregate audience results on screen: \\\\\\\"This room's biggest gap is Strategy\\\\\\\"\\\\n- CTA: \\\\\\\"Want the full diagnostic? Connect with [coach name]\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Every attendee becomes a lead in BoldEdge CRM\\\\n\\\\n### Retreat Integration (expanded from Doc 06):\\\\n- **Pre-retreat survey** \\\\u2014 team members complete individual diagnostics\\\\n- **Live session tools:**\\\\n  - Polling: \\\\\\\"What's your team's #1 challenge?\\\\\\\" (live results on screen)\\\\n  - Breakout group assignment based on diagnostic profiles\\\\n  - Commitment capture: each person enters their 90-day commitment on phone\\\\n- **Post-retreat:**\\\\n  - All commitments tracked as tasks in BoldEdge\\\\n  - 30/60/90 day automated check-ins\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"Retreat Impact Report\\\\\\\" generated at 90 days\\\\n\\\\n### Speaking Engagement Lead Capture:\\\\n- Coach gives a talk \\\\u2192 references Business Health Score\\\\n- Audience texts keyword to number \\\\u2192 gets diagnostic link\\\\n- Or scans QR \\\\u2192 same flow\\\\n- All leads tracked in BoldEdge with source tag: \\\\\\\"Event: [Name] [Date]\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 12. AI CAPABILITIES \\\\u2014 NEXT LEVEL\\\\n\\\\nBeyond what's in Docs 05-07:\\\\n\\\\n### Voice Notes \\\\u2192 Structured Data:\\\\n- Coach records voice note after session on phone\\\\n- AI transcribes \\\\u2192 extracts: key themes, action items, mood assessment, follow-up questions\\\\n- Auto-files to correct client's session record\\\\n- Coach reviews and approves \\\\u2192 action items auto-created as tasks\\\\n\\\\n### AI Coaching Debrief Partner:\\\\n- After a session, coach can \\\\\\\"debrief\\\\\\\" with AI:\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"The session with James went off track. He kept avoiding the financial review discussion.\\\\\\\"\\\\n  - AI responds: \\\\\\\"This is the 3rd time James has avoided financials. His Financial diagnostic score is 38. Consider opening next session with: 'James, I've noticed a pattern...'\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Not replacing coaching supervision \\\\u2014 augmenting it with data-backed reflection\\\\n\\\\n### Predictive Client Matching:\\\\n- When a new prospect enters (via diagnostic or referral), AI suggests:\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"This prospect profiles similarly to your most successful clients (Sarah, Mark). Recommend 1:1 Premium engagement.\\\\\\\"\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"This prospect's profile matches clients who churn within 3 months at the Starter level. Consider a deeper onboarding.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### Natural Language KPI Entry:\\\\n- Client types: \\\\\\\"Revenue was $142K this month, up from $128K last month. Hired 2 new people.\\\\\\\"\\\\n- AI parses \\\\u2192 updates KPIs: Revenue: $142,000 (+10.9%), Headcount: +2\\\\n- Coach sees updated dashboard without manual data entry\\\\n\\\\n### AI-Generated Quarterly Business Review:\\\\n- Every 90 days, AI auto-generates a comprehensive review:\\\\n  - BHS trend\\\\n  - KPI changes\\\\n  - Tasks completed and impact\\\\n  - Diagnostic score changes\\\\n  - Sessions attended\\\\n  - Wins logged\\\\n  - Recommendations accepted and status\\\\n- Coach reviews, edits, and sends to client as a branded PDF\\\\n- Saves 2-3 hours per client per quarter\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 13. FINANCIAL MODELING SUITE (For Business-Focused Coaching)\\\\n\\\\nBeyond the cash runway calculator \\\\u2014 a light financial modeling layer:\\\\n\\\\n### Break-Even Calculator:\\\\n- Inputs: Fixed costs, variable costs, average revenue per unit\\\\n- Output: \\\\\\\"You need 47 customers to break even. You currently have 38.\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Scenario: \\\\\\\"If you raise prices 15%, breakeven drops to 41 customers\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### Hiring ROI Calculator:\\\\n- \\\\\\\"If you hire a salesperson at $65K + $20K commission potential...\\\\\\\"\\\\n- \\\\\\\"They need to generate $127K in revenue to be ROI-positive\\\\\\\"\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Based on your current close rate of 22%, they need 48 leads/month\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Connected to diagnostic: \\\\\\\"Your Marketing score suggests lead generation is a gap. Fix that first.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### Pricing Optimization Modeler:\\\\n- Current pricing \\\\u2192 revenue at current volume\\\\n- \\\\\\\"What if you raised prices 10%?\\\\\\\" \\\\u2192 projected revenue assuming X% volume loss\\\\n- Industry benchmarks for price sensitivity\\\\n- Connected to Marketing & Sales diagnostic: \\\\\\\"Your value proposition score is 2/5 \\\\u2014 strengthen that before raising prices\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### Revenue Scenario Planner:\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Model: What does $500K revenue look like?\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Sliders: Price per unit, units per month, # customers, retention rate\\\\n- Output: \\\\\\\"To hit $500K, you need 42 customers paying $1,000/mo with 85% annual retention\\\\\\\"\\\\n- \\\\\\\"Your current trajectory puts you at $500K in 18 months\\\\\\\" (or identifies the gap)\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 14. LIFECYCLE REVENUE FEATURES\\\\n\\\\nNot just getting coaches and clients on \\\\u2014 maximizing revenue per relationship over time:\\\\n\\\\n### Automated Re-Engagement:\\\\n- Client goes inactive \\\\u2192 30 days later: \\\\\\\"It's been a month. Your Business Health Score was 67 \\\\u2014 want to check in?\\\\\\\"\\\\n- 90 days: \\\\\\\"3 months since your last session. See how your business has evolved.\\\\\\\"\\\\n- Annual: \\\\\\\"It's your coaching anniversary! Here's your year-in-review.\\\\\\\" (auto-generated from data)\\\\n\\\\n### Upsell Paths (Built Into the Product):\\\\n| Current State | Trigger | Upsell |\\\\n|--------------|---------|--------|\\\\n| 1:1 coaching only | Client mentions team issues | \\\\\\\"Consider adding Team Health Pulse survey\\\\\\\" |\\\\n| No 360\\\\u00b0 feedback | Client's blind spots visible in notes | \\\\\\\"360\\\\u00b0 feedback could validate this\\\\\\\" |\\\\n| Standard engagement | High BHS improvement | \\\\\\\"Your progress is exceptional. Consider a Quarterly Business Review package\\\\\\\" |\\\\n| Individual client | Client is CEO of 50+ person company | \\\\\\\"Your leadership team could benefit from group diagnostics\\\\\\\" |\\\\n| Coaching only | Client mentions board meeting | \\\\\\\"BoldEdge can generate a Board Prep Kit\\\\\\\" |\\\\n\\\\n### Package Bundling:\\\\n- Coach creates custom bundles: \\\\\\\"Growth Accelerator\\\\\\\" = 12 sessions + 2 diagnostics + 1 retreat + monthly KPI review\\\\n- Platform tracks usage against package\\\\n- Auto-alerts: \\\\\\\"Client has 2 sessions remaining in their package. Recommend bundle price: $6,500-$7,500.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### Seasonal Campaigns:\\\\n- **January:** \\\\\\\"New Year Business Health Check\\\\\\\" \\\\u2014 free diagnostic promotion for coaches to offer prospects\\\\n- **Q1:** \\\\\\\"Q1 Planning Workshop\\\\\\\" \\\\u2014 coaches get a turnkey workshop kit (slides, workbook, diagnostic link)\\\\n- **Mid-Year:** \\\\\\\"Mid-Year Business Review\\\\\\\" \\\\u2014 re-diagnostic push for all active clients\\\\n- **Q4:** \\\\\\\"Year in Review\\\\\\\" \\\\u2014 auto-generated annual summary for every client\\\\n- Each campaign comes with: email templates, social media copy, workshop materials, diagnostic links\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 15.
