ONBOARDING UX \\\\\\\\u2014 THE FIRST 5 MINUTES\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nThe #1 reason SaaS products fail is onboarding. 40-60% of free trial users never come back after day 1. BoldEdge needs the best onboarding in coaching SaaS.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Coach Onboarding Flow (First Login)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Screen 1: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"What brings you here?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"**\\\\\\\\n- [ ] I'm an executive/business coach\\\\\\\\n- [ ] I run peer groups or masterminds\\\\\\\\n- [ ] I'm exploring coaching tools\\\\\\\\n- [ ] My colleague referred me\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\u2192 Routes to personalized setup based on answer\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Screen 2: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Let's set up your practice\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" (60-second form)**\\\\\\\\n- Your name + photo\\\\\\\\n- Coaching specialty (executive, business, leadership, team, career)\\\\\\\\n- Number of active clients (0-5, 6-15, 16-30, 31+)\\\\\\\\n- Primary service (1:1, group, peer advisory, programs, mix)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\u2192 Platform pre-configures dashboard, templates, and workflows based on answers\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Screen 3: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Add your first client\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" (the activation moment)**\\\\\\\\n- Simple form: Client name, email, company name\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Send them a diagnostic?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" toggle (default ON)\\\\\\\\n- Client receives branded email invitation\\\\\\\\n- Coach sees: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Diagnostic sent! You'll see results on your dashboard when they complete it.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\u2192 **This is the \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"aha moment.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"** Coach sees the system working in real-time.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Screen 4: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"While you wait, take your own diagnostic\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"**\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"See how BoldEdge works from the client's perspective\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Coach takes the Business Health Check on their own coaching business\\\\\\\\n- Results appear in a demo dashboard\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"This is what your client will see. Now imagine 10 clients, all tracked here.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Screen 5: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"You're live. Here's your next 3 steps.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"**\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\u2705 Client invited (done)\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\u2b1c Schedule your first session (calendar link or manual)\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\u2b1c Customize your dashboard (pick which KPIs to track)\\\\\\\\n- Progress bar: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"You're 40% set up\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Client Onboarding Flow (First Login)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Step 1:** Branded email from coach: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Welcome to BoldEdge \\\\\\\\u2014 [Coach Name] has invited you\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n**Step 2:** Click \\\\\\\\u2192 lands on diagnostic (no login required for first diagnostic)\\\\\\\\n**Step 3:** Complete diagnostic (48 questions, ~10 minutes, progress bar, save & resume)\\\\\\\\n**Step 4:** Instant results page: Business Health Score + radar chart + top 3 recommendations\\\\\\\\n**Step 5:** \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Create your account to save results and access your dashboard\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n**Step 6:** Client dashboard with pre-populated data from diagnostic\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Key principle:** Client gets VALUE before they create an account. The diagnostic is the product demo.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 3. FREE TIER / FREEMIUM STRATEGY\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### What's Free Forever:\\\\\\\\n- 1 client\\\\\\\\n- Basic diagnostic (no benchmarking)\\\\\\\\n- 3 session notes\\\\\\\\n- Business Health Score\\\\\\\\n- 5 templates\\\\\\\\n- BoldEdge branding on all exports (\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Powered by BoldEdge\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\")\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### What This Achieves:\\\\\\\\n- Every coach can test the core loop (invite client \\\\\\\\u2192 run diagnostic \\\\\\\\u2192 see dashboard) with zero risk\\\\\\\\n- Free users generate diagnostic data that feeds the benchmark network\\\\\\\\n- Free users see \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Unlock benchmarking\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" / \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Add more clients\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" upgrade prompts at natural friction points\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Powered by BoldEdge\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" on free-tier exports = casual contact viral loop\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Upgrade Triggers (natural, not annoying):\\\\\\\\n- Try to add 2nd client \\\\\\\\u2192 \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Upgrade to Starter for up to 10 clients\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Try to export without branding \\\\\\\\u2192 \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Remove BoldEdge branding on Professional\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Try to access 360\\\\\\\\u00b0 feedback \\\\\\\\u2192 \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Available on Professional and above\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Complete 3 months \\\\\\\\u2192 \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"You've coached 1 client for 3 months. See your coaching effectiveness score \\\\\\\\u2014 available on Professional.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 4. MOBILE EXPERIENCE\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Coach Mobile App (Priorities):\\\\\\\\n1. **Session prep brief** \\\\\\\\u2014 swipe through client summary before a session (even in the elevator)\\\\\\\\n2. **Quick note capture** \\\\\\\\u2014 voice-to-text session notes, auto-tagged to client\\\\\\\\n3. **Task quick-add** \\\\\\\\u2014 assign a task to a client in 3 taps\\\\\\\\n4. **Notification center** \\\\\\\\u2014 overdue tasks, upcoming sessions, at-risk alerts\\\\\\\\n5. **Client quick-view** \\\\\\\\u2014 tap client name \\\\\\\\u2192 BHS score, last session, open tasks\\\\\\\\n6. **Offline mode** \\\\\\\\u2014 session notes save locally, sync when connected\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Client Mobile App (Priorities):\\\\\\\\n1. **Business Health Score** \\\\\\\\u2014 big number, front and center, swipe for trend\\\\\\\\n2. **Weekly pulse check-in** \\\\\\\\u2014 push notification Monday 8 AM, complete in 30 seconds\\\\\\\\n3. **Task list** \\\\\\\\u2014 check off completed tasks, see what's due\\\\\\\\n4. **Session reminder** \\\\\\\\u2014 with pre-session self-assessment inline\\\\\\\\n5. **Win journal** \\\\\\\\u2014 quick capture, photo attachment, voice note\\\\\\\\n6. **Habit tracker** \\\\\\\\u2014 daily check-in with streak counter\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Mobile-Specific Features:\\\\\\\\n- **Apple Watch complication** \\\\\\\\u2014 Business Health Score on watch face\\\\\\\\n- **Widget** \\\\\\\\u2014 iOS/Android home screen widget showing BHS + next session\\\\\\\\n- **Push notifications** \\\\\\\\u2014 smart timing based on user activity patterns\\\\\\\\n- **Siri/Google shortcut** \\\\\\\\u2014 \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Hey Siri, add a win to BoldEdge\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\u2192 voice capture\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 6. TRUST, COMPLIANCE & CREDIBILITY SIGNALS\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### ICF Alignment:\\\\\\\\n- Diagnostic questions aligned with ICF Core Competencies\\\\\\\\n- ROI calculation follows ICF-recommended methodology\\\\\\\\n- Platform displays: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"BoldEdge follows ICF measurement guidelines\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Future: Apply for ICF Continuing Coach Education (CCE) credits for BoldEdge training\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Security & Privacy:\\\\\\\\n- SOC 2 Type II compliance roadmap (not needed at launch, but signal intent)\\\\\\\\n- End-to-end encryption for session notes and client data\\\\\\\\n- Client data owned by coach \\\\\\\\u2014 if they leave, they export everything\\\\\\\\n- Data processing agreement (DPA) available for enterprise\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your data is never shared. Period.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" prominently on settings page\\\\\\\\n- Two-factor authentication\\\\\\\\n- Session recording stored encrypted with auto-deletion options\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Trust Signals on Every Page:\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Last backup: 2 minutes ago\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" in footer\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Data encrypted at rest and in transit\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" in security settings\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Served from [region] \\\\\\\\u2014 GDPR compliant\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" for international coaches\\\\\\\\n- Client-facing: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your responses are confidential and shared only with your coach\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Professional Credibility:\\\\\\\\n- Coach profile: display certifications (ICF ACC/PCC/MCC, DISC, EQ-i, StrengthsFinder)\\\\\\\\n- Verified badge: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"BoldEdge Verified Coach\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" (completed training + has active clients)\\\\\\\\n- Testimonial display: verified outcome badges from the platform\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Member since [date]\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" on profile\\\\\\\\n- Aggregate stats: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Has coached 23 clients on BoldEdge with average 19-point BHS improvement\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 7. API & ECOSYSTEM PLAY\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Public API (Business+ tier):\\\\\\\\n- RESTful API with full documentation\\\\\\\\n- Endpoints: clients, diagnostics, sessions, tasks, KPIs, reports\\\\\\\\n- Webhook events: diagnostic_completed, session_completed, task_completed, score_changed\\\\\\\\n- Use cases:\\\\\\\\n  - Custom dashboards in client's existing tools\\\\\\\\n  - Automated data pulls into BI platforms (Tableau, Looker, PowerBI)\\\\\\\\n  - CRM sync (push BoldEdge data to Salesforce/HubSpot)\\\\\\\\n  - Custom integrations coaches build themselves\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Embeddable Widgets:\\\\\\\\n- **Diagnostic embed** \\\\\\\\u2014 coach puts diagnostic on their own website. Client completes it \\\\\\\\u2192 data flows to BoldEdge.\\\\\\\\n  - `<iframe src=\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"boldedge.io/embed/diagnostic/[coach-id]\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" />`\\\\\\\\n  - Branded with coach's logo and colors\\\\\\\\n  - This is a massive lead-gen tool. Every coach website becomes a BoldEdge acquisition channel.\\\\\\\\n- **Business Health Score badge** \\\\\\\\u2014 client embeds their score on their own site\\\\\\\\n  - Like a \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"BBB Accredited\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" badge but for business health\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Business Health Score: 78 | Verified by BoldEdge\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  - Click \\\\\\\\u2192 public profile with score trend (if client opts in)\\\\\\\\n  - Viral loop: other businesses see it and want their own score\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Zapier/Make Integration:\\\\\\\\n- Triggers: New client, Diagnostic completed, Session completed, Task overdue, Score changed, Recommendation accepted\\\\\\\\n- Actions: Create client, Add KPI data point, Create task, Send notification\\\\\\\\n- Use cases:\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"When diagnostic is completed \\\\\\\\u2192 send Slack notification to coach\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"When task is overdue by 3 days \\\\\\\\u2192 send reminder via Twilio SMS\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"When new client added \\\\\\\\u2192 create Google Drive folder\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"When session completed \\\\\\\\u2192 log to Airtable tracking sheet\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 8. ADVANCED GAMIFICATION\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nBeyond badges \\\\\\\\u2014 gamification that drives real behavioral change:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### For Clients:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Levels System:**\\\\\\\\n- Level 1: Newcomer (completed intake)\\\\\\\\n- Level 2: Committed (completed first diagnostic)\\\\\\\\n- Level 3: In Motion (completed 3 sessions + 5 tasks)\\\\\\\\n- Level 4: Gaining Traction (BHS improved 10+ points)\\\\\\\\n- Level 5: Transforming (BHS improved 20+ points)\\\\\\\\n- Level 6: High Performer (BHS above 75)\\\\\\\\n- Level 7: Elite (BHS above 85 for 3+ months)\\\\\\\\n- Level 8: Champion (BHS above 90 + refers another client)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nEach level unlocks:\\\\\\\\n- A visual badge on their dashboard\\\\\\\\n- A shareable achievement graphic\\\\\\\\n- Access to progressively advanced resources\\\\\\\\n- At Level 5+: invitation to join a peer group of similar-level clients\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Streaks:**\\\\\\\\n- Weekly pulse check-in streak: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"12 weeks in a row! \\\\\\\\ud83d\\\\\\\\udd25\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Task completion streak: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Perfect month \\\\\\\\u2014 all tasks completed on time!\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Session attendance streak: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"6 consecutive sessions attended\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Broken streaks trigger gentle re-engagement: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"You missed your check-in this week. Take 30 seconds to get back on track.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Challenges:**\\\\\\\\n- Coach assigns a 30-day challenge: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Delegate one decision per day for 30 days\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Client checks in daily\\\\\\\\n- Completion = badge + highlight in next session prep\\\\\\\\n- Peer group challenges: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Which group member can improve their Financial score the most in 90 days?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### For Coaches:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Coaching Milestones:**\\\\\\\\n- 10th client onboarded\\\\\\\\n- 100th session completed\\\\\\\\n- 50th diagnostic run\\\\\\\\n- First client reaches BHS 80+\\\\\\\\n- 5 clients with positive ROI verified\\\\\\\\n- 1,000 coaching hours logged\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Leaderboard (opt-in, anonymized):**\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your average client BHS improvement: 18 points (top 25% of BoldEdge coaches)\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your client retention rate: 88% (top 15%)\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your task completion rate: 85% (above average)\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Not competitive \\\\\\\\u2014 more like \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"here's where you stand\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" benchmarking\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 9. WHITE-LABEL DEEP MECHANICS\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nFor Enterprise tier ($499-999/mo) \\\\\\\\u2014 what makes white-label actually valuable:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Visual Customization:\\\\\\\\n- Custom logo (replaces BoldEdge logo everywhere)\\\\\\\\n- Custom color scheme (primary, secondary, accent)\\\\\\\\n- Custom fonts\\\\\\\\n- Custom email templates (header, footer, colors)\\\\\\\\n- Custom domain: `coaching.mereditheicher.com`\\\\\\\\n- Custom favicon\\\\\\\\n- Removable \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Powered by BoldEdge\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" footer\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Content Customization:\\\\\\\\n- Custom diagnostic questions (add, remove, reword)\\\\\\\\n- Custom scoring weights\\\\\\\\n- Custom recommendation library\\\\\\\\n- Custom templates (replace defaults with coach's own)\\\\\\\\n- Custom onboarding flow text\\\\\\\\n- Custom email copy\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Branding Deliverables:\\\\\\\\n- All PDF exports use coach's branding\\\\\\\\n- Client portal shows coach's branding only\\\\\\\\n- Proposals and agreements branded\\\\\\\\n- Diagnostic emails branded\\\\\\\\n- ROI reports branded\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### What BoldEdge Still Powers (Under the Hood):\\\\\\\\n- All infrastructure and hosting\\\\\\\\n- AI intelligence and benchmarking\\\\\\\\n- Workflow automation engine\\\\\\\\n- Data storage and security\\\\\\\\n- Platform updates and new features\\\\\\\\n- Support and maintenance\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Pitch to coaches:** \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your brand. Our brain. Give your clients a world-class technology experience with your name on it.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 10. COACH SUCCESS SYSTEM (Reduce Coach Churn)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nThe biggest risk to BoldEdge is coach churn. If coaches leave, everything stops. Build systems to make coaches successful:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Coach Onboarding Checklist (First 30 Days):\\\\\\\\n- [ ] Week 1: Complete profile, take personal diagnostic, add first client\\\\\\\\n- [ ] Week 2: Client completes diagnostic, coach reviews results, first session prep used\\\\\\\\n- [ ] Week 3: First post-session follow-up workflow runs, 2+ tasks assigned\\\\\\\\n- [ ] Week 4: Second client added OR second session with first client\\\\\\\\n- Progress tracked on \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Coach Success Dashboard\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" (internal admin view)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Automated Coach Health Monitoring:\\\\\\\\n- **Green:** Coach has 3+ active clients, runs sessions weekly, uses diagnostic\\\\\\\\n- **Yellow:** Coach has 1-2 clients, sessions sporadic, some features unused\\\\\\\\n- **Red:** Coach hasn't logged in 14+ days, no sessions in 30 days\\\\\\\\n- Automated nudges for Yellow/Red:\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"You haven't run a diagnostic in 3 weeks. Want to send one to [client name]?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Most BoldEdge coaches see retention improve after using session prep briefs. Try it before your next session.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Coach Community:\\\\\\\\n- Private forum/Slack for BoldEdge coaches\\\\\\\\n- Monthly webinar: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"BoldEdge Best Practices\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" featuring a power user\\\\\\\\n- Template sharing: coaches contribute templates to a shared library\\\\\\\\n- Peer matching: connect coaches in non-competing markets for knowledge exchange\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Coach of the Month\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" spotlight\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Coach Training Resources:\\\\\\\\n- Video library: How to use every feature (2-3 min each)\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"The BoldEdge Method\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\u2014 a coaching methodology built around the diagnostic\\\\\\\\n  - Step 1: Diagnostic \\\\\\\\u2192 Baseline\\\\\\\\n  - Step 2: Priority Gap Identification \\\\\\\\u2192 Focus Areas\\\\\\\\n  - Step 3: Coaching Plan \\\\\\\\u2192 90-Day Objectives\\\\\\\\n  - Step 4: Session Cadence \\\\\\\\u2192 Weekly/Bi-weekly\\\\\\\\n  - Step 5: Task & Action Loop \\\\\\\\u2192 Between-Session Accountability\\\\\\\\n  - Step 6: Re-Diagnostic \\\\\\\\u2192 Measure Progress\\\\\\\\n  - Step 7: ROI Report \\\\\\\\u2192 Prove Value\\\\\\\\n  - Step 8: Renewal \\\\\\\\u2192 Continue Engagement\\\\\\\\n- Certification program (as described in Doc 06)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 11. EVENT & OFFLINE INTEGRATION\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nCoaching doesn't only happen online. BoldEdge should bridge digital and in-person:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Conference/Workshop Mode:\\\\\\\\n- Coach runs a workshop at a conference\\\\\\\\n- Audience scans QR code \\\\\\\\u2192 takes mini-diagnostic (10 questions) on phone\\\\\\\\n- Instant results on their phone\\\\\\\\n- Coach shows aggregate audience results on screen: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"This room's biggest gap is Strategy\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- CTA: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Want the full diagnostic? Connect with [coach name]\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Every attendee becomes a lead in BoldEdge CRM\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Retreat Integration (expanded from Doc 06):\\\\\\\\n- **Pre-retreat survey** \\\\\\\\u2014 team members complete individual diagnostics\\\\\\\\n- **Live session tools:**\\\\\\\\n  - Polling: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"What's your team's #1 challenge?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" (live results on screen)\\\\\\\\n  - Breakout group assignment based on diagnostic profiles\\\\\\\\n  - Commitment capture: each person enters their 90-day commitment on phone\\\\\\\\n- **Post-retreat:**\\\\\\\\n  - All commitments tracked as tasks in BoldEdge\\\\\\\\n  - 30/60/90 day automated check-ins\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Retreat Impact Report\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" generated at 90 days\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Speaking Engagement Lead Capture:\\\\\\\\n- Coach gives a talk \\\\\\\\u2192 references Business Health Score\\\\\\\\n- Audience texts keyword to number \\\\\\\\u2192 gets diagnostic link\\\\\\\\n- Or scans QR \\\\\\\\u2192 same flow\\\\\\\\n- All leads tracked in BoldEdge with source tag: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Event: [Name] [Date]\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 12. AI CAPABILITIES \\\\\\\\u2014 NEXT LEVEL\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nBeyond what's in Docs 05-07:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Voice Notes \\\\\\\\u2192 Structured Data:\\\\\\\\n- Coach records voice note after session on phone\\\\\\\\n- AI transcribes \\\\\\\\u2192 extracts: key themes, action items, mood assessment, follow-up questions\\\\\\\\n- Auto-files to correct client's session record\\\\\\\\n- Coach reviews and approves \\\\\\\\u2192 action items auto-created as tasks\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### AI Coaching Debrief Partner:\\\\\\\\n- After a session, coach can \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"debrief\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" with AI:\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"The session with James went off track. He kept avoiding the financial review discussion.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  - AI responds: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"This is the 3rd time James has avoided financials. His Financial diagnostic score is 38. Consider opening next session with: 'James, I've noticed a pattern...'\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Not replacing coaching supervision \\\\\\\\u2014 augmenting it with data-backed reflection\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Predictive Client Matching:\\\\\\\\n- When a new prospect enters (via diagnostic or referral), AI suggests:\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"This prospect profiles similarly to your most successful clients (Sarah, Mark). Recommend 1:1 Premium engagement.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n  - \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"This prospect's profile matches clients who churn within 3 months at the Starter level. Consider a deeper onboarding.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Natural Language KPI Entry:\\\\\\\\n- Client types: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Revenue was $142K this month, up from $128K last month. Hired 2 new people.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- AI parses \\\\\\\\u2192 updates KPIs: Revenue: $142,000 (+10.9%), Headcount: +2\\\\\\\\n- Coach sees updated dashboard without manual data entry\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### AI-Generated Quarterly Business Review:\\\\\\\\n- Every 90 days, AI auto-generates a comprehensive review:\\\\\\\\n  - BHS trend\\\\\\\\n  - KPI changes\\\\\\\\n  - Tasks completed and impact\\\\\\\\n  - Diagnostic score changes\\\\\\\\n  - Sessions attended\\\\\\\\n  - Wins logged\\\\\\\\n  - Recommendations accepted and status\\\\\\\\n- Coach reviews, edits, and sends to client as a branded PDF\\\\\\\\n- Saves 2-3 hours per client per quarter\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 13. FINANCIAL MODELING SUITE (For Business-Focused Coaching)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nBeyond the cash runway calculator \\\\\\\\u2014 a light financial modeling layer:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Break-Even Calculator:\\\\\\\\n- Inputs: Fixed costs, variable costs, average revenue per unit\\\\\\\\n- Output: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"You need 47 customers to break even. You currently have 38.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Scenario: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"If you raise prices 15%, breakeven drops to 41 customers\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Hiring ROI Calculator:\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"If you hire a salesperson at $65K + $20K commission potential...\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"They need to generate $127K in revenue to be ROI-positive\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Based on your current close rate of 22%, they need 48 leads/month\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Connected to diagnostic: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your Marketing score suggests lead generation is a gap. Fix that first.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Pricing Optimization Modeler:\\\\\\\\n- Current pricing \\\\\\\\u2192 revenue at current volume\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"What if you raised prices 10%?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\u2192 projected revenue assuming X% volume loss\\\\\\\\n- Industry benchmarks for price sensitivity\\\\\\\\n- Connected to Marketing & Sales diagnostic: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your value proposition score is 2/5 \\\\\\\\u2014 strengthen that before raising prices\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Revenue Scenario Planner:\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Model: What does $500K revenue look like?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Sliders: Price per unit, units per month, # customers, retention rate\\\\\\\\n- Output: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"To hit $500K, you need 42 customers paying $1,000/mo with 85% annual retention\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your current trajectory puts you at $500K in 18 months\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" (or identifies the gap)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 14. LIFECYCLE REVENUE FEATURES\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nNot just getting coaches and clients on \\\\\\\\u2014 maximizing revenue per relationship over time:\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Automated Re-Engagement:\\\\\\\\n- Client goes inactive \\\\\\\\u2192 30 days later: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"It's been a month. Your Business Health Score was 67 \\\\\\\\u2014 want to check in?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- 90 days: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"3 months since your last session. Companies that maintain coaching see 2.4x better outcomes.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- 6 months: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Time for your semi-annual diagnostic? See how your business has evolved.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Annual: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"It's your coaching anniversary! Here's your year-in-review.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" (auto-generated from data)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Upsell Paths (Built Into the Product):\\\\\\\\n| Current State | Trigger | Upsell |\\\\\\\\n|--------------|---------|--------|\\\\\\\\n| 1:1 coaching only | Client mentions team issues | \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Consider adding Team Health Pulse survey\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" |\\\\\\\\n| No 360\\\\\\\\u00b0 feedback | Client's blind spots visible in notes | \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"360\\\\\\\\u00b0 feedback could validate this\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" |\\\\\\\\n| Standard engagement | High BHS improvement | \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your progress is exceptional. Consider a Quarterly Business Review package\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" |\\\\\\\\n| Individual client | Client is CEO of 50+ person company | \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your leadership team could benefit from group diagnostics\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" |\\\\\\\\n| Coaching only | Client mentions board meeting | \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"BoldEdge can generate a Board Prep Kit\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" |\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Package Bundling:\\\\\\\\n- Coach creates custom bundles: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Growth Accelerator\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" = 12 sessions + 2 diagnostics + 1 retreat + monthly KPI review\\\\\\\\n- Platform tracks usage against package\\\\\\\\n- Auto-alerts: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Client has 2 sessions remaining in their package. Renewal conversation recommended.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Suggested pricing based on component value: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"This bundle's component value is $8,500. Recommend bundle price: $6,500-$7,500.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Seasonal Campaigns:\\\\\\\\n- **January:** \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"New Year Business Health Check\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\u2014 free diagnostic promotion for coaches to offer prospects\\\\\\\\n- **Q1:** \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Q1 Planning Workshop\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\u2014 coaches get a turnkey workshop kit (slides, workbook, diagnostic link)\\\\\\\\n- **Mid-Year:** \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Mid-Year Business Review\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\u2014 re-diagnostic push for all active clients\\\\\\\\n- **Q4:** \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Year in Review\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\\u2014 auto-generated annual summary for every client\\\\\\\\n- Each campaign comes with: email templates, social media copy, workshop materials, diagnostic links\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 15. ADMIN & PLATFORM INTELLIGENCE (For Ned)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Product Analytics Dashboard:\\\\\\\\n- Feature adoption: which features are used, how often, by whom\\\\\\\\n- Activation funnel: signup \\\\\\\\u2192 first client \\\\\\\\u2192 first diagnostic \\\\\\\\u2192 first session \\\\\\\\u2192 paid conversion\\\\\\\\n- Cohort analysis: which month's signups retained best? Why?\\\\\\\\n- Revenue by tier, by feature, by geography\\\\\\\\n- NPS by segment\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Churn Prediction:\\\\\\\\n- ML model trained on: login frequency, feature usage, client count, session frequency, support tickets\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Coach Jane has a 72% probability of churning in the next 30 days\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Risk factors: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Hasn't logged in 12 days, only 1 active client, never used session prep\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Recommended intervention: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Personal outreach + offer onboarding call\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### A/B Testing Framework:\\\\\\\\n- Test onboarding flows, pricing pages, upgrade prompts, email templates\\\\\\\\n- Track: conversion rate, activation rate, retention at 30/60/90 days\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Onboarding Flow B (with personal diagnostic) has 34% higher activation than Flow A\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n### Partner Dashboard:\\\\\\\\n- Meredith (and future partners): see their referrals, commissions, client activity\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"This month: 3 referrals, 2 converted, $297 in commissions\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Your referral link was clicked 47 times. 12 started trials. 2 converted.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n- Leaderboard of top partners\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 16. De-risks decision. Below \"approval threshold\" for most solo coaches.\n- **$149 Professional** \u2192 The \"real\" tier. This is where 60-70% of paying coaches land. Session prep AI brief is the killer feature that justifies the jump. A coach billing $300/hr who saves 20 min/session pays for the entire month in one session prep.\n- **$349 Business** \u2192 For coaches running a real business. Financial modeling, email marketing, API, and team diagnostics justify the jump. At 25+ clients billing $500/mo avg, this is <3% of revenue.\n- **$799+ Enterprise** \u2192 White-label, multi-coach, custom everything. Priced on value, not cost. A 5-coach firm billing $50K/mo pays 1.6% for their entire technology stack.\n\n### Revenue Math\n\n| Scenario | Coaches | Mix | MRR | ARR |\n|----------|---------|-----|-----|-----|\n| Year 1 (Month 12) | 50 paying | 30% Starter, 50% Pro, 15% Biz, 5% Ent | $6,080 | $72,960 |\n| Year 2 (Month 24) | 200 paying | 25% Starter, 50% Pro, 20% Biz, 5% Ent | $27,600 | $331,200 |\n| Year 3 (Month 36) | 500 paying | 20% Starter, 45% Pro, 25% Biz, 10% Ent | $82,050 | $984,600 |\n\n**Assumptions:** Avg revenue per coach: Starter $49, Pro $149, Business $349, Enterprise $899. tested\n- [ ] SSL certificate valid\n- [ ] Load testing passed (50 concurrent users minimum)\n\n---\n\n## 3. MULTI-COACH FIRM MODE\n\n### The Opportunity\nSolo coaches are the first market, but coaching firms (5-50 coaches) represent 10x the revenue per account. BoldEdge needs a firm-level experience from Business tier up.\n\n### Firm Architecture\n\n```\n\u250c\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2510\n\u2502           FIRM ADMIN DASHBOARD          \u2502\n\u2502  (Firm owner/manager sees everything)   \u2502\n\u251c\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2524\n\u2502                                         \u2502\n\u2502  \u250c\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2510  \u250c\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2510  \u250c\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2510\n\u2502  \u2502 Coach A   \u2502  \u2502 Coach B   \u2502  \u2502 Coach \u2502\n\u2502  \u2502 12 clients\u2502  \u2502 8 clients \u2502  \u2502 C ... \u2502\n\u2502  \u2502 $14K MRR  \u2502  \u2502 $9K MRR   \u2502  \u2502       \u2502\n\u2502  \u2514\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2518  \u2514\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2518  \u2514\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2518\n\u2502                                         \u2502\n\u2502  FIRM-LEVEL VIEWS:                      \u2502\n\u2502  \u2022 Total clients: 47                    \u2502\n\u2502  \u2022 Total MRR: $52,000                   \u2502\n\u2502  \u2022 Avg BHS improvement: 19 points       \u2502\n\u2502  \u2022 Avg client retention: 84%            \u2502\n\u2502  \u2022 Top performing coach: Coach A        \u2502\n\u2502  \u2022 At-risk clients: 3                   \u2502\n\u2514\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2518\n```\n\n### Firm Admin Features\n\n**Dashboard:**\n- Aggregate metrics across all coaches: total clients, total revenue, avg BHS improvement, retention rate, session count\n- Coach comparison view (opt-in): who's most effective, who has capacity, who needs support\n- Client list with filter: by coach, by status, by risk level, by BHS range\n- Revenue tracking: MRR by coach, pipeline by coach, renewal calendar\n\n**Coach Management:**\n- Add/remove coaches (each at $99/mo per seat)\n- Assign clients to coaches (drag-and-drop or auto-assign based on fit)\n- Transfer clients between coaches (with history preserved)\n- Coach performance metrics: session adherence, task completion rates, client outcomes\n- Coach activity log: last login, sessions this week, diagnostics run\n\n**Client Assignment Logic:**\n- Manual: firm admin assigns\n- Smart match: based on coach specialty, capacity, client industry, location\n- Load balancing: \"Coach A has 15 clients, Coach C has 4. Recommend routing new clients to Coach C.\"\n- Re-assignment workflow: when a coach leaves, clients auto-queue for reassignment\n\n**Firm-Level Reporting:**\n- \"Firm Impact Report\" \u2014 quarterly aggregate: clients served, outcomes achieved, ROI generated\n- Exportable for firm marketing: \"Our firm improved average client BHS by 22 points\"\n- Individual coach reports for performance reviews\n- Client satisfaction aggregated across firm\n\n**Firm Billing:**\n- Single invoice for all seats\n- Volume discount: 5-9 seats = 10% off, 10-19 seats = 15% off, 20+ seats = 20% off\n- Coach can have individual plan OR be part of firm plan (no double-billing)\n\n### Firm Pricing\n\n| Firm Size | Base | Per Coach Seat | Total | Per Coach Savings |\n|-----------|------|---------------|-------|-------------------|\n| 2-4 coaches | $349 (Business) | $99/each additional | $547-$646 | ~$162/coach |\n| 5-9 coaches | $349 | $89/each (10% off) | $703-$1,061 | ~$118/coach |\n| 10-19 coaches | $349 | $84/each (15% off) | $1,105-$1,861 | ~$98/coach |\n| 20+ coaches | Custom | $79/each (20% off) | Custom | ~$79/coach |\n\n---\n\n## 4. CORPORATE BUYER PORTAL\n\n### The Second Market\nBeyond solo coaches and firms, there's a massive B2B market: companies buying coaching for their leaders.\n\n**Buyers:** HR directors, L&D managers, Chief People Officers, CEOs of mid-market companies (100-1,000 employees)\n\n**What They Buy:** Coaching engagements for 5-50 leaders simultaneously\n\n**What They Need (That Coaches Don't):**\n- ROI reporting rolled up across all leaders being coached\n- Compliance and confidentiality guarantees\n- Single invoice and procurement-friendly pricing\n- Progress visibility without seeing session content\n- Vendor management features\n\n### Corporate Dashboard\n\n```\n\u250c\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2510\n\u2502         ACME CORP \u2014 COACHING PROGRAM        \u2502\n\u2502  HR Admin: Sarah Johnson                     \u2502\n\u251c\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2524\n\u2502                                              \u2502\n\u2502  PROGRAM OVERVIEW                            \u2502\n\u2502  Leaders enrolled: 12                        \u2502\n\u2502  Coaches assigned: 3                         \u2502\n\u2502  Avg sessions completed: 6.2                 \u2502\n\u2502  Program start: Jan 15, 2026                 \u2502\n\u2502  Program end: Jun 15, 2026                   \u2502\n\u2502                                              \u2502\n\u2502  AGGREGATE OUTCOMES (no individual names)    \u2502\n\u2502  Avg BHS improvement: +16 points             \u2502\n\u2502  Diagnostic completion: 100%                 \u2502\n\u2502  Session attendance: 94%                     \u2502\n\u2502  Task completion: 78%                        \u2502\n\u2502  Program NPS: 8.7/10                         \u2502\n\u2502                                              \u2502\n\u2502  INVESTMENT & ROI                            \u2502\n\u2502  Total investment: $72,000                                          \u2502\n\u2502  \u26a0 ATTENTION NEEDED                          \u2502\n\u2502  \u2022 2 leaders haven't scheduled next session  \u2502\n\u2502  \u2022 1 coach has capacity for 2 more leaders   \u2502\n\u2502  \u2022 Mid-program survey due in 2 weeks         \u2502\n\u2502                                              \u2502\n\u2502  [\ud83d\udcca Download Program Report]                \u2502\n\u2502  [\ud83d\udce7 Share with Executive Team]              \u2502\n\u2514\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2518\n```\n\n### Key Design Principle: CONFIDENTIALITY WALL\n\nThe corporate buyer sees:\n- \u2705 Aggregate metrics (average BHS improvement, completion rates)\n- \u2705 Program-level ROI\n- \u2705 Attendance and engagement data\n- \u2705 Anonymous feedback themes\n\nThe corporate buyer NEVER sees:\n- \u274c Individual session notes\n- \u274c Individual diagnostic responses\n- \u274c What any specific leader said\n- \u274c Individual KPIs or financial data\n- \u274c Coach's notes about a specific leader\n\nThis confidentiality wall is the ONLY way coaching programs work in corporate settings. Coaches won't use a platform that compromises it, and leaders won't be honest in sessions.\n\n### Corporate Features\n\n**Procurement:**\n- Custom SOW generator (scope, timeline, deliverables, pricing)\n- W-9/vendor registration auto-complete\n- Single PO for entire program\n- Invoicing on net-30/60 terms\n- Budget tracking vs. approved PO\n\n**Program Management:**\n- Cohort creation: \"Q1 2026 Leadership Development \u2014 12 leaders\"\n- Coach assignment: match coaches to leaders based on specialty/preference\n- Milestone tracking: enrollment \u2192 diagnostic \u2192 sessions \u2192 mid-point review \u2192 final assessment\n- Automated reminders to leaders who fall behind\n- Mid-program and end-of-program surveys\n\n**Reporting (For HR/L&D):**\n- Monthly progress report (automated)\n- Mid-program assessment with recommendations\n- Final program impact report (board-ready)\n- Year-over-year comparison (for recurring programs)\n- Benchmark against other companies (anonymized): \"Your leaders improved 16 points vs. industry avg 14\"\n\n### Corporate Pricing\n\n| Program Size | Per Leader/Month | Minimum Commitment | Includes |\n|-------------|-----------------|-------------------|----------|\n| 5-10 leaders | $399/leader/mo | 6 months | Full diagnostic, sessions, reporting, admin portal |\n| 11-25 leaders | $349/leader/mo | 6 months | + dedicated CSM, custom reporting |\n| 26-50 leaders | $299/leader/mo | 12 months | + executive briefings, custom diagnostic |\n| 50+ leaders | Custom | 12 months | Full enterprise, white-label option |\n\n**Revenue example:** 15 leaders \u00d7 $349/mo \u00d7 12 months = $62,820/year from ONE corporate account\n\n### The Coach's Cut\nWhen BoldEdge sells corporate programs:\n- Coach delivers sessions (their usual rate)\n- BoldEdge provides platform + admin + reporting\n- Split: Coach gets 70% of per-leader fee, BoldEdge gets 30%\n- Or: Coach pays for BoldEdge subscription, bills corporate client directly at their own rate\n\n---\n\n## 5. INFRASTRUCTURE & OPERATIONS\n\n### Technology Stack (Recommended)\n\n| Component | Choice | Rationale |\n|-----------|--------|-----------|\n| **Frontend** | React + Next.js | SEO for marketing pages, fast for app |\n| **Backend** | Node.js (Express or Fastify) | Same language as frontend, fast dev |\n| **Database** | PostgreSQL (Supabase or Neon) | Relational data, excellent for SaaS |\n| **Auth** | Clerk or Auth0 | SSO, MFA, social login, not worth building |\n| **Payments** | Stripe | Subscriptions, invoicing, metered billing |\n| **Email** | Resend or Postmark | Transactional. Mailgun for marketing volume |\n| **File Storage** | AWS S3 or Cloudflare R2 | Documents, exports, attachments |\n| **Hosting** | Vercel (frontend) + Railway/Render (backend) | Fast deploys, auto-scaling, reasonable cost |\n| **AI** | OpenAI API (GPT-4o) or Claude API | Session prep, recommendations, NLP |\n| **Search** | Algolia or Meilisearch | Coach directory, template library, in-app search |\n| **Analytics** | PostHog (self-hosted) or Mixpanel | Product analytics, feature adoption |\n| **Error Monitoring** | Sentry | Crash reporting, performance monitoring |\n| **Uptime Monitoring** | BetterUptime or UptimeRobot | Status page, alerting |\n| **CI/CD** | GitHub Actions | Build, test, deploy |\n| **CDN** | Cloudflare | Speed, DDoS protection, edge caching |\n\n### Monthly Infrastructure Cost Estimate\n\n| Stage | Users | Monthly Cost | Notes |\n|-------|-------|-------------|-------|\n| MVP / Beta | <50 coaches | $50-$150/mo | Free tiers of most services |\n| Early Growth | 50-200 coaches | $200-$500/mo | Paid tiers, moderate traffic |\n| Growth | 200-500 coaches | $500-$1,500/mo | More compute, more storage, more email |\n| Scale | 500-2,000 coaches | $1,500-$5,000/mo | Dedicated resources, CDN, support tools |\n\n### Database Schema \u2014 Core Tables\n\n```sql\n-- Core entities\ncoaches           -- id, email, name, plan, firm_id, created_at\nclients           -- id, coach_id, name, email, company, industry, status\nfirms             -- id, name, owner_coach_id, plan, seats\ncorporate_programs -- id, company_name, hr_admin_id, start_date, end_date\n\n-- Diagnostic engine\ndiagnostics       -- id, client_id, type, status, started_at, completed_at\ndiagnostic_responses -- id, diagnostic_id, question_id, answer_value\ndiagnostic_scores -- id, diagnostic_id, category, score, benchmark_percentile\nquestions         -- id, category, text, weight, industry_module\n\n-- Sessions\nsessions          -- id, client_id, coach_id, date, status, duration_min\nsession_notes     -- id, session_id, content, ai_summary, created_at\nsession_preps     -- id, session_id, ai_brief, reviewed_by_coach\n\n-- Tasks & accountability\ntasks             -- id, client_id, assigned_by, title, due_date, status\nhabits            -- id, client_id, title, frequency, streak_count\nwins              -- id, client_id, description, date, category\npulse_checkins    -- id, client_id, date, energy, progress, blockers\n\n-- KPIs & financials\nkpis              -- id, client_id, metric_name, value, date, source\nkpi_definitions   -- id, name, category, unit, direction (higher_better)\n\n-- Business tools\nproposals         -- id, coach_id, client_id, status, total_value\npackages          -- id, coach_id, name, sessions_included, price\npipeline_leads    -- id, coach_id, name, email, stage, source, score\ntestimonials      -- id, coach_id, client_id, text, permission_level\n\n-- Analytics\nevents            -- id, user_id, event_type, properties, timestamp\ncoach_health      -- id, coach_id, status (green/yellow/red), factors, date\n```\n\n### Security Requirements\n\n| Requirement | Implementation | Priority |\n|-------------|---------------|----------|\n| Encryption at rest | PostgreSQL encryption + S3 SSE | Launch |\n| Encryption in transit | TLS 1.3 everywhere | Launch |\n| Authentication | MFA available, enforced for Enterprise | Launch |\n| Session management | JWT with refresh tokens, 24hr expiry | Launch |\n| Input validation | Server-side validation on all endpoints | Launch |\n| RBAC | Role-based access: admin, coach, client, firm_admin, corp_admin | Launch |\n| Data backup | Daily automated backups, 30-day retention | Launch |\n| Audit log | All data access logged with timestamps | Month 2 |\n| Penetration testing | Annual third-party pen test | Month 6 |\n| SOC 2 Type I | Begin compliance process | Month 9 |\n| SOC 2 Type II | Complete certification | Month 18 |\n| GDPR compliance | Data export, deletion, DPA available | Launch |\n\n### Monitoring & Alerting\n\n| What | Tool | Alert Threshold |\n|------|------|----------------|\n| Uptime | BetterUptime | Any downtime \u2192 immediate |\n| Error rate | Sentry | >1% error rate \u2192 Slack alert |\n| Response time | Built-in middleware | p95 >500ms \u2192 warning, >2s \u2192 critical |\n| Database | Supabase dashboard | CPU >80%, storage >75% |\n| Email delivery | Resend dashboard | Bounce rate >5% |\n| Payment failures | Stripe webhooks | Any failed charge \u2192 alert + retry |\n| Security | Cloudflare | Unusual traffic patterns \u2192 alert |\n\n### Backup & Recovery\n\n| Data | Backup Frequency | Retention | Recovery Time |\n|------|-----------------|-----------|---------------|\n| Database (full) | Daily | 30 days | <1 hour |\n| Database (incremental) | Every 6 hours | 7 days | <30 min |\n| File storage | Continuous (S3 versioning) | 90 days | <15 min |\n| Code | Git (GitHub) | Forever | <5 min (redeploy) |\n| Configuration | Environment variables in Vault/Doppler | Versioned | <10 min |\n\n---\n\n## 6. LEGAL TEMPLATES\n\n### 6A. Terms of Service (Key Provisions)\n\n**Account Terms:**\n- Coaches must be 18+ and provide accurate information\n- One person per account (no sharing credentials)\n- Coach is responsible for maintaining account security\n- BoldEdge reserves right to suspend accounts violating ToS\n\n**Payment Terms:**\n- Monthly billing on subscription anniversary\n- Annual plans billed upfront with stated discount\n- Failed payments: 3 retry attempts over 10 days, then account downgraded to Free\n- No refunds for partial months; annual plans refundable pro-rata within first 30 days\n- Price changes: 30-day notice for monthly, honored through current annual term\n\n**Data & Privacy:**\n- Coach owns their data and client data\n- BoldEdge may use anonymized, aggregated data for benchmarking (no PII)\n- Client data is confidential between coach and client\n- BoldEdge will not access individual session notes except for technical support (with coach permission)\n- Full data export available at any time\n- Data deleted 30 days after account termination (coach can request earlier)\n\n**Acceptable Use:**\n- No illegal activity, harassment, spam, or abuse\n- No scraping, reverse engineering, or unauthorized API access\n- No reselling access without Enterprise agreement\n- No misrepresenting BoldEdge as coach's own proprietary technology (except white-label)\n\n**Liability:**\n- BoldEdge is a technology platform, not a coaching service\n- BoldEdge does not provide legal, financial, or therapeutic advice\n- AI recommendations are informational, not professional advice\n- Coach is solely responsible for coaching quality and client relationships\n- Standard SaaS limitation of liability (damages capped at 12 months of fees)\n\n**Termination:**\n- Coach can cancel anytime; access continues through end of billing period\n- BoldEdge can terminate for ToS violations with 30-day notice (immediate for egregious violations)\n- Data export available for 30 days post-termination\n\n### 6B. Privacy Policy (Key Provisions)\n\n**Data Collected:**\n- Account information (name, email, company, payment method)\n- Client information (as entered by coach)\n- Diagnostic responses and scores\n- Session metadata (date, duration \u2014 NOT content unless opted in)\n- Usage data (features used, login frequency, page views)\n- Device/browser information (for support and security)\n\n**Data Usage:**\n- Provide and improve the platform\n- Generate anonymized benchmarks and reports\n- Send transactional emails (notifications, billing)\n- Send marketing emails (opt-out available)\n- Prevent fraud and ensure security\n- Comply with legal obligations\n\n**Data NOT Shared With:**\n- Advertisers (BoldEdge does not sell data)\n- Other coaches (unless coach explicitly shares)\n- Corporate buyers (session content is confidential)\n- Third parties for marketing\n\n**Data Shared With:**\n- Service providers (hosting, email, payment) under DPA\n- Law enforcement (only when legally compelled)\n- In aggregate (anonymized benchmarks, no PII)\n\n**Client Rights:**\n- Access: request copy of all data\n- Correction: update inaccurate data\n- Deletion: request deletion of account and data\n- Portability: export data in standard formats\n- Objection: opt out of marketing, benchmarking use\n\n**Retention:**\n- Active accounts: data retained for duration of account\n- Post-termination: 30 days then permanent deletion\n- Anonymized aggregate data: retained indefinitely (no PII)\n- Backups: purged within 90 days of deletion request\n\n### 6C. Coach-Client Confidentiality Agreement Template\n\n```\nCOACHING CONFIDENTIALITY AGREEMENT\n\nThis agreement is between [Coach Name] (\"Coach\") and [Client Name] (\"Client\").\n\n1. CONFIDENTIAL INFORMATION\nAll information shared during coaching sessions, diagnostics, assessments,\nand related communications is considered confidential. This includes but\nis not limited to: business financials, strategic plans, personal\ninformation, employee matters, and diagnostic results.\n\n2. COACH OBLIGATIONS\nThe Coach agrees to:\n- Maintain strict confidentiality of all client information\n- Not disclose client information to any third party without written consent\n- Store client data securely using encrypted systems (BoldEdge platform)\n- Destroy or return confidential information upon request\n\n3. EXCEPTIONS\nConfidentiality may be broken only if:\n- Client provides written consent to share specific information\n- Required by law or court order\n- There is imminent risk of harm to the client or others\n- Anonymous, aggregate data is used for benchmarking (no PII)\n\n4. DATA PLATFORM\nCoaching data is stored on the BoldEdge platform. Client acknowledges:\n- Data is encrypted at rest and in transit\n- Coach controls access to all client data\n- Client can request data export or deletion at any time\n- Anonymized diagnostic data may contribute to industry benchmarks\n\n5. DURATION\nThis agreement survives the end of the coaching engagement indefinitely.\n\nSigned: _________________________ Date: _________\nCoach: [Coach Name]\n\nSigned: _________________________ Date: _________\nClient: [Client Name]\n```\n\n### 6D. Coaching Services Agreement Template\n\n```\nCOACHING SERVICES AGREEMENT\n\nBetween: [Coach Name/Company] (\"Coach\")\nAnd: [Client Name/Company] (\"Client\")\nEffective Date: [Date]\n\n1. SERVICES\nCoach will provide [executive/business/leadership] coaching services including:\n- [X] sessions per [month/quarter] of [60/90] minutes each\n- [X] diagnostic assessments (initial + re-diagnostic at [interval])\n- [  ] 360\u00b0 feedback assessment\n- [  ] Team diagnostic\n- [  ] Quarterly Business Review\n- Between-session support via [email/text/platform messaging]\n\n2. SCHEDULE\n- Sessions: [weekly/bi-weekly/monthly] on [day/time] or as mutually agreed\n- Location: [in-person/virtual/hybrid]\n- Cancellation: 24-hour notice required; late cancellations count as used session\n\n3. INVESTMENT\n- Total engagement value: $[amount]\n- Payment schedule: $[amount] per [month/quarter]\n- Payment method: [invoice/auto-charge]\n- Payment terms: Due within [15/30] days of invoice\n\n4. TERM & RENEWAL\n- Initial term: [3/6/12] months from effective date\n- Renewal: [auto-renews / requires new agreement]\n- Early termination: Either party may terminate with 30 days written notice\n- Unused sessions: [non-refundable / refundable pro-rata / rollover 30 days]\n\n5. CONFIDENTIALITY\nPer attached Confidentiality Agreement (incorporated by reference).\n\n6. EXPECTATIONS\nCoach will: provide professional coaching, maintain confidentiality,\nprepare for sessions, track progress via BoldEdge platform.\n\nClient will: attend sessions, complete assigned tasks and diagnostics,\ncommunicate openly, provide timely payment.\n\n7. LIMITATIONS\nCoaching is not therapy, consulting, or legal/financial advice.\nCoach will refer client to appropriate professionals when needed.\n\n8. TECHNOLOGY\nSessions supported by BoldEdge coaching platform. Client will:\n- Complete diagnostic assessments via the platform\n- Review and update tasks and KPIs as agreed\n- Access their dashboard for progress tracking\n\n9. INTELLECTUAL PROPERTY\n- Frameworks and methodologies remain property of Coach\n- Client's business data remains property of Client\n- Platform data shared per BoldEdge Terms of Service\n\nSigned: _________________________ Date: _________\nCoach: [Coach Name]\n\nSigned: _________________________ Date: _________\nClient: [Client Name]\n```\n\n### 6E. Data Processing Agreement (DPA) \u2014 Summary Provisions\n\nFor Enterprise/corporate clients who require it:\n\n- **Data Controller:** Coach (or Corporate Client)\n- **Data Processor:** BoldEdge\n- **Sub-processors:** Listed (Supabase, AWS, Stripe, email provider) with notification of changes\n- **Data location:** United States (with option for EU hosting at Enterprise tier)\n- **Processing purpose:** Solely to provide the BoldEdge platform services\n- **Security measures:** Encryption, access controls, monitoring, backups, incident response\n- **Breach notification:** Within 72 hours of confirmed breach\n- **Data deletion:** Within 30 days of termination or upon request\n- **Audit rights:** Corporate client may request annual compliance attestation\n- **Standard Contractual Clauses:** Included for international data transfers\n\n### 6F. Referral Partner Agreement Template\n\n```\nBOLDEDGE REFERRAL PARTNER AGREEMENT\n\nBetween: BoldEdge LLC (\"BoldEdge\")\nAnd: [Partner Name] (\"Partner\")\n\n1. REFERRAL PROGRAM\nPartner will refer potential coaches to BoldEdge via:\n- Unique referral link: boldedge.io/r/[partner-code]\n- Direct introduction to BoldEdge team\n- Co-marketing activities (webinars, content, events)\n\n2. COMPENSATION\n- First 6 months: 15% of referred coach's monthly subscription\n- Months 7-12: 10% of referred coach's monthly subscription\n- After 12 months: 5% ongoing (for life of the referred account)\n- Payments: Monthly, net-30, via Stripe Connect or ACH\n- Minimum payout: $50 (rolls over if below threshold)\n\n3. ATTRIBUTION\n- Referral tracked via unique link or manual attribution\n- 90-day cookie/attribution window\n- If prospect uses referral link and signs up within 90 days = credited\n\n4. EXCLUSIONS\n- Self-referrals not eligible\n- Existing BoldEdge users not eligible\n- Fraudulent referrals result in immediate termination\n\n5. TERM\n- 12 months, auto-renewing\n- Either party may terminate with 30 days notice\n- Commissions earned before termination will be paid\n\n6. RESPONSIBILITIES\nPartner will: accurately represent BoldEdge, not make false claims,\ncomply with applicable advertising laws.\nBoldEdge will: track referrals, process payments, provide marketing materials.\n```\n\n---\n\n## 7. DATA MIGRATION & ONBOARDING FROM COMPETITORS\n\n### CSV Import Wizard\n\nMost coaches switching from other tools have data in spreadsheets or other platforms. BoldEdge needs a dead-simple import.\n\n**Supported Import Sources:**\n- CSV/Excel files (universal)\n- CoachAccountable export\n- Paperbell export\n- Satori export\n- Practice Better export\n- Google Sheets\n\n**Import Workflow:**\n1. Coach uploads file (drag-and-drop)\n2. BoldEdge auto-detects columns: \"We found: Name, Email, Company, Start Date, Notes\"\n3. Coach maps columns to BoldEdge fields (smart suggestions pre-filled)\n4. Preview: \"We'll import 12 clients with their contact info and session history\"\n5. Import: data created, coach sees populated dashboard\n6. Review: \"Import complete! 12 clients added. 2 had missing emails \u2014 want to fix those?\"\n\n**What Can Be Imported:**\n- Client contact information\n- Session history (dates, durations, notes if available)\n- Goals and action items\n- Documents and attachments\n- Financial data (invoices, payments)\n\n**What Can't Be Imported (But BoldEdge Replaces):**\n- Diagnostic data (client takes BoldEdge diagnostic \u2014 this IS the product demo)\n- Benchmarks (generated by BoldEdge's proprietary data)\n- AI insights (generated fresh from BoldEdge's intelligence)\n\n### Competitor Comparison Landing Pages\n\nFor SEO and conversion:\n- \"BoldEdge vs CoachAccountable\" \u2014 feature comparison table\n- \"BoldEdge vs Paperbell\" \u2014 feature comparison table\n- \"BoldEdge vs Practice Better\" \u2014 feature comparison table\n- \"Switching from [Competitor] to BoldEdge\" \u2014 step-by-step guide with import instructions\n\n---\n\n## 8. COACHING SUPERVISION MODULE\n\n### Why This Matters\nICF requires coaches to complete supervised hours for credentialing (ACC, PCC, MCC). Currently this happens via phone calls or Zoom with a mentor coach reviewing session recordings or written notes. BoldEdge can make this better.\n\n### How It Works\n\n**Coach (Supervisee) Side:**\n- Selects sessions to share with supervisor (anonymized client names optional)\n- Shares: session notes, diagnostic context, AI debrief, task outcomes\n- Adds reflection: \"What I'm struggling with...\" \"Where I want feedback...\"\n- Logs supervision hours toward ICF credentialing\n\n**Supervisor Side:**\n- Reviews shared session materials asynchronously or in live supervision session\n- Provides feedback: text annotations on session notes, ratings on ICF competencies\n- Tracks supervisee progress: competency development over time\n- Issues supervision verification (for ICF credentialing documentation)\n\n**BoldEdge Facilitates:**\n- Supervision scheduling (built into calendar)\n- Supervision notes (separate from coaching notes \u2014 meta-level)\n- Competency tracking: which ICF competencies are developing, which need work\n- Hour logging: \"42 of 100 required mentor coaching hours completed\"\n- Export: formal supervision documentation for ICF credentialing applications\n\n### Revenue\n- Supervisor subscription: $49/mo (special tier for mentor coaches)\n- Supervisee access: included in Professional+ plans\n- ICF credentialing tracking: free (drives adoption)\n\n### Competitive Advantage\nNo coaching platform does this well. It creates a built-in training & development layer that keeps coaches on the platform from the moment they start their coaching career through credentialing and beyond.\n\n---\n\n## 9. COACH MATCHING MARKETPLACE\n\n### Phase 1: Directory (Launch)\n- Coach creates public profile: name, photo, bio, specialties, industries, certifications, location\n- Verified badges: \"BoldEdge Verified\" (completed training + active clients)\n- Aggregate metrics: \"22 clients coached, avg 19-point BHS improvement\"\n- Searchable by: specialty, industry, location, price range, certification\n- URL: boldedge.io/coaches/[name]\n\n### Phase 2: Matching (Month 6+)\n- Prospect takes free diagnostic \u2192 gets results + \"Want a coach? Here are 3 matches.\"\n- Matching algorithm: industry experience, price range, coaching style, location, availability\n- Prospect requests intro \u2192 coach gets notification with prospect profile + diagnostic results\n- Coach accepts/declines within 48 hours\n- Discovery call scheduled through BoldEdge\n\n### Phase 3: Marketplace (Month 12+)\n- Prospect books directly through BoldEdge\n- BoldEdge handles: scheduling, payment, invoicing\n- Platform fee: 15% of first 6 months, 10% ongoing\n- Coach sees: \"New client from BoldEdge marketplace \u2014 net revenue: $425/session\"\n- Reviews and ratings from clients (verified, post-engagement only)\n\n### Marketplace Revenue at Scale\n- 500 coaches \u00d7 avg 1 marketplace client each/year \u00d7 $500/mo \u00d7 12.5% avg fee = $375,000/year\n- This is pure platform revenue with zero customer acquisition cost\n\n---\n\n## DOCUMENT SUMMARY\n\n| Section | Key Deliverable |\n|---------|----------------|\n| Pricing & Packaging | MULTI-COACH FIRM MODE\\n\\n### The Opportunity\\nSolo coaches are the first market, but coaching firms (5-50 coaches) represent 10x the revenue per account. BoldEdge needs a firm-level experience from Business tier up.\\n\\n### Firm Architecture\\n\\n```\\n\\u250c\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2510\\n\\u2502 Firm Admin Features\\n\\n**Dashboard:**\\n- Aggregate metrics across all coaches: total clients, total revenue, avg BHS improvement, retention rate, session count\\n- Coach comparison view (opt-in): who's most effective, who has capacity, who needs support\\n- Client list with filter: by coach, by status, by risk level, by BHS range\\n- Revenue tracking: MRR by coach, pipeline by coach, renewal calendar\\n\\n**Coach Management:**\\n- Add/remove coaches (each at $99/mo per seat)\\n- Assign clients to coaches (drag-and-drop or auto-assign based on fit)\\n- Transfer clients between coaches (with history preserved)\\n- Coach performance metrics: session adherence, task completion rates, client outcomes\\n- Coach activity log: last login, sessions this week, diagnostics run\\n\\n**Client Assignment Logic:**\\n- Manual: firm admin assigns\\n- Smart match: based on coach specialty, capacity, client industry, location\\n- Load balancing: \\\"Coach A has 15 clients, Coach C has 4. Recommend routing new clients to Coach C.\\\"\\n- Re-assignment workflow: when a coach leaves, clients auto-queue for reassignment\\n\\n**Firm-Level Reporting:**\\n- \\\"Firm Impact Report\\\" \\u2014 quarterly aggregate: clients served, outcomes achieved, ROI generated\\n- Exportable for firm marketing: \\\"Our firm improved average client BHS by 22 points\\\"\\n- Individual coach reports for performance reviews\\n- Client satisfaction aggregated across firm\\n\\n**Firm Billing:**\\n- Single invoice for all seats\\n- Volume discount: 5-9 seats = 10% off, 10-19 seats = 15% off, 20+ seats = 20% off\\n- Coach can have individual plan OR be part of firm plan (no double-billing)\\n\\n### Firm Pricing\\n\\n| Firm Size | Base | Per Coach Seat | Total | Per Coach Savings |\\n|-----------|------|---------------|-------|-------------------|\\n| 2-4 coaches | $349 (Business) | $99/each additional | $547-$646 | ~$162/coach |\\n| 5-9 coaches | $349 | $89/each (10% off) | $703-$1,061 | ~$118/coach |\\n| 10-19 coaches | $349 | $84/each (15% off) | $1,105-$1,861 | ~$98/coach |\\n| 20+ coaches | Custom | $79/each (20% off) | Custom | ~$79/coach |\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 4. CORPORATE BUYER PORTAL\\n\\n### The Second Market\\nBeyond solo coaches and firms, there's a massive B2B market: companies buying coaching for their leaders.\\n\\n**Buyers:** HR directors, L&D managers, Chief People Officers, CEOs of mid-market companies (100-1,000 employees)\\n\\n**What They Buy:** Coaching engagements for 5-50 leaders simultaneously\\n\\n**What They Need (That Coaches Don't):**\\n- ROI reporting rolled up across all leaders being coached\\n- Compliance and confidentiality guarantees\\n- Single invoice and procurement-friendly pricing\\n- Progress visibility without seeing session content\\n- Vendor management features\\n\\n### Corporate Dashboard\\n\\n```\\n\\u250c\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2510\\n\\u2502 Key Design Principle: CONFIDENTIALITY WALL\\n\\nThe corporate buyer sees:\\n- \\u2705 Aggregate metrics (average BHS improvement, completion rates)\\n- \\u2705 Program-level ROI\\n- \\u2705 Attendance and engagement data\\n- \\u2705 Anonymous feedback themes\\n\\nThe corporate buyer NEVER sees:\\n- \\u274c Individual session notes\\n- \\u274c Individual diagnostic responses\\n- \\u274c What any specific leader said\\n- \\u274c Individual KPIs or financial data\\n- \\u274c Coach's notes about a specific leader\\n\\nThis confidentiality wall is the ONLY way coaching programs work in corporate settings. Coaches won't use a platform that compromises it, and leaders won't be honest in sessions.\\n\\n### Corporate Features\\n\\n**Procurement:**\\n- Custom SOW generator (scope, timeline, deliverables, pricing)\\n- W-9/vendor registration auto-complete\\n- Single PO for entire program\\n- Invoicing on net-30/60 terms\\n- Budget tracking vs. approved PO\\n\\n**Program Management:**\\n- Cohort creation: \\\"Q1 2026 Leadership Development \\u2014 12 leaders\\\"\\n- Coach assignment: match coaches to leaders based on specialty/preference\\n- Milestone tracking: enrollment \\u2192 diagnostic \\u2192 sessions \\u2192 mid-point review \\u2192 final assessment\\n- Automated reminders to leaders who fall behind\\n- Mid-program and end-of-program surveys\\n\\n**Reporting (For HR/L&D):**\\n- Monthly progress report (automated)\\n- Mid-program assessment with recommendations\\n- Final program impact report (board-ready)\\n- Year-over-year comparison (for recurring programs)\\n- Benchmark against other companies (anonymized): \\\"Your leaders improved 16 points vs. industry avg 14\\\"\\n\\n### Corporate Pricing\\n\\n| Program Size | Per Leader/Month | Minimum Commitment | Includes |\\n|-------------|-----------------|-------------------|----------|\\n| 5-10 leaders | $399/leader/mo | 6 months | Full diagnostic, sessions, reporting, admin portal |\\n| 11-25 leaders | $349/leader/mo | 6 months | + dedicated CSM, custom reporting |\\n| 26-50 leaders | $299/leader/mo | 12 months | + executive briefings, custom diagnostic |\\n| 50+ leaders | Custom | 12 months | Full enterprise, white-label option |\\n\\n**Revenue example:** 15 leaders \\u00d7 $349/mo \\u00d7 12 months = $62,820/year from ONE corporate account\\n\\n### The Coach's Cut\\nWhen BoldEdge sells corporate programs:\\n- Coach delivers sessions (their usual rate)\\n- BoldEdge provides platform + admin + reporting\\n- Split: Coach gets 70% of per-leader fee, BoldEdge gets 30%\\n- Or: Coach pays for BoldEdge subscription, bills corporate client directly at their own rate\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 5. INFRASTRUCTURE & OPERATIONS\\n\\n### Technology Stack (Recommended)\\n\\n| Component | Choice | Rationale |\\n|-----------|--------|-----------|\\n| **Frontend** | React + Next.js | SEO for marketing pages, fast for app |\\n| **Backend** | Node.js (Express or Fastify) | Same language as frontend, fast dev |\\n| **Database** | PostgreSQL (Supabase or Neon) | Relational data, excellent for SaaS |\\n| **Auth** | Clerk or Auth0 | SSO, MFA, social login, not worth building |\\n| **Payments** | Stripe | Subscriptions, invoicing, metered billing |\\n| **Email** | Resend or Postmark | Transactional. Mailgun for marketing volume |\\n| **File Storage** | AWS S3 or Cloudflare R2 | Documents, exports, attachments |\\n| **Hosting** | Vercel (frontend) + Railway/Render (backend) | Fast deploys, auto-scaling, reasonable cost |\\n| **AI** | OpenAI API (GPT-4o) or Claude API | Session prep, recommendations, NLP |\\n| **Search** | Algolia or Meilisearch | Coach directory, template library, in-app search |\\n| **Analytics** | PostHog (self-hosted) or Mixpanel | Product analytics, feature adoption |\\n| **Error Monitoring** | Sentry | Crash reporting, performance monitoring |\\n| **Uptime Monitoring** | BetterUptime or UptimeRobot | Status page, alerting |\\n| **CI/CD** | GitHub Actions | Build, test, deploy |\\n| **CDN** | Cloudflare | Speed, DDoS protection, edge caching |\\n\\n### Monthly Infrastructure Cost Estimate\\n\\n| Stage | Users | Monthly Cost | Notes |\\n|-------|-------|-------------|-------|\\n| MVP / Beta | <50 coaches | $50-$150/mo | Free tiers of most services |\\n| Early Growth | 50-200 coaches | $200-$500/mo | Paid tiers, moderate traffic |\\n| Growth | 200-500 coaches | $500-$1,500/mo | More compute, more storage, more email |\\n| Scale | 500-2,000 coaches | $1,500-$5,000/mo | Dedicated resources, CDN, support tools |\\n\\n### Database Schema \\u2014 Core Tables\\n\\n```sql\\n-- Core entities\\ncoaches           -- id, email, name, plan, firm_id, created_at\\nclients           -- id, coach_id, name, email, company, industry, status\\nfirms             -- id, name, owner_coach_id, plan, seats\\ncorporate_programs -- id, company_name, hr_admin_id, start_date, end_date\\n\\n-- Diagnostic engine\\ndiagnostics       -- id, client_id, type, status, started_at, completed_at\\ndiagnostic_responses -- id, diagnostic_id, question_id, answer_value\\ndiagnostic_scores -- id, diagnostic_id, category, score, benchmark_percentile\\nquestions         -- id, category, text, weight, industry_module\\n\\n-- Sessions\\nsessions          -- id, client_id, coach_id, date, status, duration_min\\nsession_notes     -- id, session_id, content, ai_summary, created_at\\nsession_preps     -- id, session_id, ai_brief, reviewed_by_coach\\n\\n-- Tasks & accountability\\ntasks             -- id, client_id, assigned_by, title, due_date, status\\nhabits            -- id, client_id, title, frequency, streak_count\\nwins              -- id, client_id, description, date, category\\npulse_checkins    -- id, client_id, date, energy, progress, blockers\\n\\n-- KPIs & financials\\nkpis              -- id, client_id, metric_name, value, date, source\\nkpi_definitions   -- id, name, category, unit, direction (higher_better)\\n\\n-- Business tools\\nproposals         -- id, coach_id, client_id, status, total_value\\npackages          -- id, coach_id, name, sessions_included, price\\npipeline_leads    -- id, coach_id, name, email, stage, source, score\\ntestimonials      -- id, coach_id, client_id, text, permission_level\\n\\n-- Analytics\\nevents            -- id, user_id, event_type, properties, timestamp\\ncoach_health      -- id, coach_id, status (green/yellow/red), factors, date\\n```\\n\\n### Security Requirements\\n\\n| Requirement | Implementation | Priority |\\n|-------------|---------------|----------|\\n| Encryption at rest | PostgreSQL encryption + S3 SSE | Launch |\\n| Encryption in transit | TLS 1.3 everywhere | Launch |\\n| Authentication | MFA available, enforced for Enterprise | Launch |\\n| Session management | JWT with refresh tokens, 24hr expiry | Launch |\\n| Input validation | Server-side validation on all endpoints | Launch |\\n| RBAC | Role-based access: admin, coach, client, firm_admin, corp_admin | Launch |\\n| Data backup | Daily automated backups, 30-day retention | Launch |\\n| Audit log | All data access logged with timestamps | Month 2 |\\n| Penetration testing | Annual third-party pen test | Month 6 |\\n| SOC 2 Type I | Begin compliance process | Month 9 |\\n| SOC 2 Type II | Complete certification | Month 18 |\\n| GDPR compliance | Data export, deletion, DPA available | Launch |\\n\\n### Monitoring & Alerting\\n\\n| What | Tool | Alert Threshold |\\n|------|------|----------------|\\n| Uptime | BetterUptime | Any downtime \\u2192 immediate |\\n| Error rate | Sentry | >1% error rate \\u2192 Slack alert |\\n| Response time | Built-in middleware | p95 >500ms \\u2192 warning, >2s \\u2192 critical |\\n| Database | Supabase dashboard | CPU >80%, storage >75% |\\n| Email delivery | Resend dashboard | Bounce rate >5% |\\n| Payment failures | Stripe webhooks | Any failed charge \\u2192 alert + retry |\\n| Security | Cloudflare | Unusual traffic patterns \\u2192 alert |\\n\\n### Backup & Recovery\\n\\n| Data | Backup Frequency | Retention | Recovery Time |\\n|------|-----------------|-----------|---------------|\\n| Database (full) | Daily | 30 days | <1 hour |\\n| Database (incremental) | Every 6 hours | 7 days | <30 min |\\n| File storage | Continuous (S3 versioning) | 90 days | <15 min |\\n| Code | Git (GitHub) | Forever | <5 min (redeploy) |\\n| Configuration | Environment variables in Vault/Doppler | Versioned | <10 min |\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 6. LEGAL TEMPLATES\\n\\n### 6A. Terms of Service (Key Provisions)\\n\\n**Account Terms:**\\n- Coaches must be 18+ and provide accurate information\\n- One person per account (no sharing credentials)\\n- Coach is responsible for maintaining account security\\n- BoldEdge reserves right to suspend accounts violating ToS\\n\\n**Payment Terms:**\\n- Monthly billing on subscription anniversary\\n- Annual plans billed upfront with stated discount\\n- Failed payments: 3 retry attempts over 10 days, then account downgraded to Free\\n- No refunds for partial months; annual plans refundable pro-rata within first 30 days\\n- Price changes: 30-day notice for monthly, honored through current annual term\\n\\n**Data & Privacy:**\\n- Coach owns their data and client data\\n- BoldEdge may use anonymized, aggregated data for benchmarking (no PII)\\n- Client data is confidential between coach and client\\n- BoldEdge will not access individual session notes except for technical support (with coach permission)\\n- Full data export available at any time\\n- Data deleted 30 days after account termination (coach can request earlier)\\n\\n**Acceptable Use:**\\n- No illegal activity, harassment, spam, or abuse\\n- No scraping, reverse engineering, or unauthorized API access\\n- No reselling access without Enterprise agreement\\n- No misrepresenting BoldEdge as coach's own proprietary technology (except white-label)\\n\\n**Liability:**\\n- BoldEdge is a technology platform, not a coaching service\\n- BoldEdge does not provide legal, financial, or therapeutic advice\\n- AI recommendations are informational, not professional advice\\n- Coach is solely responsible for coaching quality and client relationships\\n- Standard SaaS limitation of liability (damages capped at 12 months of fees)\\n\\n**Termination:**\\n- Coach can cancel anytime; access continues through end of billing period\\n- BoldEdge can terminate for ToS violations with 30-day notice (immediate for egregious violations)\\n- Data export available for 30 days post-termination\\n\\n### 6B. Privacy Policy (Key Provisions)\\n\\n**Data Collected:**\\n- Account information (name, email, company, payment method)\\n- Client information (as entered by coach)\\n- Diagnostic responses and scores\\n- Session metadata (date, duration \\u2014 NOT content unless opted in)\\n- Usage data (features used, login frequency, page views)\\n- Device/browser information (for support and security)\\n\\n**Data Usage:**\\n- Provide and improve the platform\\n- Generate anonymized benchmarks and reports\\n- Send transactional emails (notifications, billing)\\n- Send marketing emails (opt-out available)\\n- Prevent fraud and ensure security\\n- Comply with legal obligations\\n\\n**Data NOT Shared With:**\\n- Advertisers (BoldEdge does not sell data)\\n- Other coaches (unless coach explicitly shares)\\n- Corporate buyers (session content is confidential)\\n- Third parties for marketing\\n\\n**Data Shared With:**\\n- Service providers (hosting, email, payment) under DPA\\n- Law enforcement (only when legally compelled)\\n- In aggregate (anonymized benchmarks, no PII)\\n\\n**Client Rights:**\\n- Access: request copy of all data\\n- Correction: update inaccurate data\\n- Deletion: request deletion of account and data\\n- Portability: export data in standard formats\\n- Objection: opt out of marketing, benchmarking use\\n\\n**Retention:**\\n- Active accounts: data retained for duration of account\\n- Post-termination: 30 days then permanent deletion\\n- Anonymized aggregate data: retained indefinitely (no PII)\\n- Backups: purged within 90 days of deletion request\\n\\n### 6C. Coach-Client Confidentiality Agreement Template\\n\\n```\\nCOACHING CONFIDENTIALITY AGREEMENT\\n\\nThis agreement is between [Coach Name] (\\\"Coach\\\") and [Client Name] (\\\"Client\\\").\\n\\n1. CONFIDENTIAL INFORMATION\\nAll information shared during coaching sessions, diagnostics, assessments,\\nand related communications is considered confidential. This includes but\\nis not limited to: business financials, strategic plans, personal\\ninformation, employee matters, and diagnostic results.\\n\\n2. COACH OBLIGATIONS\\nThe Coach agrees to:\\n- Maintain strict confidentiality of all client information\\n- Not disclose client information to any third party without written consent\\n- Store client data securely using encrypted systems (BoldEdge platform)\\n- Destroy or return confidential information upon request\\n\\n3. EXCEPTIONS\\nConfidentiality may be broken only if:\\n- Client provides written consent to share specific information\\n- Required by law or court order\\n- There is imminent risk of harm to the client or others\\n- Anonymous, aggregate data is used for benchmarking (no PII)\\n\\n4. DATA PLATFORM\\nCoaching data is stored on the BoldEdge platform. Client acknowledges:\\n- Data is encrypted at rest and in transit\\n- Coach controls access to all client data\\n- Client can request data export or deletion at any time\\n- Anonymized diagnostic data may contribute to industry benchmarks\\n\\n5. DURATION\\nThis agreement survives the end of the coaching engagement indefinitely.\\n\\nSigned: _________________________ Date: _________\\nCoach: [Coach Name]\\n\\nSigned: _________________________ Date: _________\\nClient: [Client Name]\\n```\\n\\n### 6D.
