INTELLECTUAL PROPERTY\\n- Frameworks and methodologies remain property of Coach\\n- Client's business data remains property of Client\\n- Platform data shared per BoldEdge Terms of Service\\n\\nSigned: _________________________ Date: _________\\nCoach: [Coach Name]\\n\\nSigned: _________________________ Date: _________\\nClient: [Client Name]\\n```\\n\\n### 6E. Data Processing Agreement (DPA) \\u2014 Summary Provisions\\n\\nFor Enterprise/corporate clients who require it:\\n\\n- **Data Controller:** Coach (or Corporate Client)\\n- **Data Processor:** BoldEdge\\n- **Sub-processors:** Listed (Supabase, AWS, Stripe, email provider) with notification of changes\\n- **Data location:** United States (with option for EU hosting at Enterprise tier)\\n- **Processing purpose:** Solely to provide the BoldEdge platform services\\n- **Security measures:** Encryption, access controls, monitoring, backups, incident response\\n- **Breach notification:** Within 72 hours of confirmed breach\\n- **Data deletion:** Within 30 days of termination or upon request\\n- **Audit rights:** Corporate client may request annual compliance attestation\\n- **Standard Contractual Clauses:** Included for international data transfers\\n\\n### 6F. Referral Partner Agreement Template\\n\\n```\\nBOLDEDGE REFERRAL PARTNER AGREEMENT\\n\\nBetween: BoldEdge LLC (\\\"BoldEdge\\\")\\nAnd: [Partner Name] (\\\"Partner\\\")\\n\\n1. REFERRAL PROGRAM\\nPartner will refer potential coaches to BoldEdge via:\\n- Unique referral link: boldedge.io/r/[partner-code]\\n- Direct introduction to BoldEdge team\\n- Co-marketing activities (webinars, content, events)\\n\\n2. COMPENSATION\\n- First 6 months: 15% of referred coach's monthly subscription\\n- Months 7-12: 10% of referred coach's monthly subscription\\n- After 12 months: 5% ongoing (for life of the referred account)\\n- Payments: Monthly, net-30, via Stripe Connect or ACH\\n- Minimum payout: $50 (rolls over if below threshold)\\n\\n3. ATTRIBUTION\\n- Referral tracked via unique link or manual attribution\\n- 90-day cookie/attribution window\\n- If prospect uses referral link and signs up within 90 days = credited\\n\\n4. EXCLUSIONS\\n- Self-referrals not eligible\\n- Existing BoldEdge users not eligible\\n- Fraudulent referrals result in immediate termination\\n\\n5. TERM\\n- 12 months, auto-renewing\\n- Either party may terminate with 30 days notice\\n- Commissions earned before termination will be paid\\n\\n6. RESPONSIBILITIES\\nPartner will: accurately represent BoldEdge, not make false claims,\\ncomply with applicable advertising laws.\\nBoldEdge will: track referrals, process payments, provide marketing materials.\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 7. DATA MIGRATION & ONBOARDING FROM COMPETITORS\\n\\n### CSV Import Wizard\\n\\nMost coaches switching from other tools have data in spreadsheets or other platforms. BoldEdge needs a dead-simple import.\\n\\n**Supported Import Sources:**\\n- CSV/Excel files (universal)\\n- CoachAccountable export\\n- Paperbell export\\n- Satori export\\n- Practice Better export\\n- Google Sheets\\n\\n**Import Workflow:**\\n1. Coach uploads file (drag-and-drop)\\n2. BoldEdge auto-detects columns: \\\"We found: Name, Email, Company, Start Date, Notes\\\"\\n3. Coach maps columns to BoldEdge fields (smart suggestions pre-filled)\\n4. Preview: \\\"We'll import 12 clients with their contact info and session history\\\"\\n5. Import: data created, coach sees populated dashboard\\n6. \u2500\u2500\u2500 INSTALL BANNER \u2500\u2500\u2500 -->\n<div class=\"install-banner\" id=\"installBanner\">\n  <div class=\"icon\">\ud83d\udcf2</div>\n  <div class=\"text\">\n    <div \u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\n// INDEXEDDB \u2014 OFFLINE-FIRST DATA LAYER\n// \u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\n\nconst DB_NAME = 'boldedge';\nconst DB_VERSION = 1;\nlet db = null;\n\nfunction openDB() {\n  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n    const req = indexedDB.open(DB_NAME, DB_VERSION);\n    req.onupgradeneeded = (e) => {\n      const d = e.target.result;\n      if (!d.objectStoreNames.contains('clients')) {\n        d.createObjectStore('clients', { keyPath: 'id' });\n      }\n      if (!d.objectStoreNames.contains('tasks')) {\n        const ts = d.createObjectStore('tasks', { keyPath: 'id' });\n        ts.createIndex('clientId', 'clientId');\n        ts.createIndex('status', 'status');\n      }\n      if (!d.objectStoreNames.contains('wins')) {\n        d.createObjectStore('wins', { keyPath: 'id' });\n      }\n      if (!d.objectStoreNames.contains('sessions')) {\n        d.createObjectStore('sessions', { keyPath: 'id' });\n      }\n      if (!d.objectStoreNames.contains('diagnostics')) {\n        d.createObjectStore('diagnostics', { keyPath: 'id' });\n      }\n      if (!d.objectStoreNames.contains('notes')) {\n        d.createObjectStore('notes', { keyPath: 'id' });\n      }\n      if (!d.objectStoreNames.contains('pulseCheckins')) {\n        d.createObjectStore('pulseCheckins', { keyPath: 'id' });\n      }\n      if (!d.objectStoreNames.contains('syncQueue')) {\n        const sq = d.createObjectStore('syncQueue', { keyPath: 'id', autoIncrement: true });\n        sq.createIndex('synced', 'synced');\n      }\n      if (!d.objectStoreNames.contains('meta')) {\n        d.createObjectStore('meta', { keyPath: 'key' });\n      }\n    };\n    req.onsuccess = (e) => { db = e.target.result; resolve(db); };\n    req.onerror = (e) => reject(e);\n  });\n}\n\nfunction dbPut(store, data) {\n  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n    const tx = db.transaction(store, 'readwrite');\n    tx.objectStore(store).put(data);\n    tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();\n    tx.onerror = (e) => reject(e);\n  });\n}\n\nfunction dbGetAll(store) {\n  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n    const tx = db.transaction(store, 'readonly');\n    const req = tx.objectStore(store).getAll();\n    req.onsuccess = () => resolve(req.result);\n    req.onerror = (e) => reject(e);\n  });\n}\n\nfunction dbDelete(store, id) {\n  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n    const tx = db.transaction(store, 'readwrite');\n    tx.objectStore(store).delete(id);\n    tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();\n    tx.onerror = (e) => reject(e);\n  });\n}\n\nfunction dbCount(store) {\n  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n    const tx = db.transaction(store, 'readonly');\n    const req = tx.objectStore(store).count();\n    req.onsuccess = () => resolve(req.result);\n    req.onerror = (e) => reject(e);\n  });\n}\n\n// Queue changes for sync\nasync function queueChange(action, store, data) {\n  await dbPut('syncQueue', {\n    action, store, data,\n    timestamp: Date.now(),\n    synced: 0,\n    deviceId: getDeviceId()\n  });\n  updatePendingCount();\n}\n\n// \u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\n// DEVICE ID & DISCOVERY\n// \u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\n\nfunction getDeviceId() {\n  let id = localStorage.getItem('boldedge_device_id');\n  if (!id) {\n    id = 'dev_' + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9);\n    localStorage.setItem('boldedge_device_id', id);\n  }\n  return id;\n}\n\n// \u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\n// WIFI SYNC \u2014 WebSocket-based local network sync\n// \u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\n\nlet syncWs = null;\nlet syncConnected = false;\nconst SYNC_PORT = 3579;\n\nclass LocalSync {\n  constructor() {\n    this.ws = null;\n    this.isServer = false;\n    this.connected = false;\n    this.peers = new Set();\n    this.serverUrl = null;\n  }\n\n  // Try connecting to a sync server on the local network\n  async connect(serverHost) {\n    if (this.ws) this.ws.close();\n\n    const url = serverHost || `ws://${location.hostname}:${SYNC_PORT}`;\n    this.serverUrl = url;\n\n    try {\n      this.ws = new WebSocket(url);\n\n      this.ws.onopen = () => {\n        this.connected = true;\n        syncConnected = true;\n        updateSyncUI('connected');\n        // Send our current state hash\n        this.ws.send(JSON.stringify({\n          type: 'HELLO',\n          deviceId: getDeviceId(),\n          timestamp: Date.now()\n        }));\n        // Flush pending changes\n        this.flushQueue();\n      };\n\n      this.ws.onmessage = async (evt) => {\n        try {\n          const msg = JSON.parse(evt.data);\n          await this.handleMessage(msg);\n        } catch (e) {\n          console.error('[Sync] Message parse error:', e);\n        }\n      };\n\n      this.ws.onclose = () => {\n        this.connected = false;\n        syncConnected = false;\n        updateSyncUI('offline');\n        // Auto-reconnect after 5s\n        setTimeout(() => this.connect(serverHost), 5000);\n      };\n\n      this.ws.onerror = () => {\n        updateSyncUI('offline');\n      };\n\n    } catch (e) {\n      updateSyncUI('offline');\n    }\n  }\n\n  async handleMessage(msg) {\n    switch (msg.type) {\n      case 'SYNC_DATA':\n        // Received data from another device\n        if (msg.store && msg.data) {\n          await dbPut(msg.store, msg.data);\n          refreshCurrentPage();\n          showToast('\ud83d\udce5', 'Synced from ' + (msg.deviceId || 'peer'));\n        }\n        break;\n\n      case 'SYNC_BATCH':\n        // Received batch of changes\n        if (msg.changes) {\n          for (const change of msg.changes) {\n            if (change.action === 'put') {\n              await dbPut(change.store, change.data);\n            } else if (change.action === 'delete') {\n              await dbDelete(change.store, change.data.id);\n            }\n          }\n          refreshCurrentPage();\n          showToast('\ud83d\udce5', `Synced ${msg.changes.length} changes`);\n        }\n        break;\n\n      case 'REQUEST_FULL_SYNC':\n        // Peer wants all our data\n        await this.sendFullState();\n        break;\n\n      case 'FULL_STATE':\n        // Received complete state from peer\n        if (msg.stores) {\n          for (const [storeName, records] of Object.entries(msg.stores)) {\n            for (const record of records) {\n              await dbPut(storeName, record);\n            }\n          }\n          refreshCurrentPage();\n          showToast('\u2705', 'Full sync complete');\n        }\n        break;\n    }\n\n    updateLastSync();\n  }\n\n  async flushQueue() {\n    if (!this.connected || !this.ws) return;\n\n    const pending = await dbGetAll('syncQueue');\n    const unsynced = pending.filter(p => !p.synced);\n\n    if (unsynced.length === 0) return;\n\n    updateSyncUI('syncing');\n\n    this.ws.send(JSON.stringify({\n      type: 'SYNC_BATCH',\n      changes: unsynced.map(p => ({\n        action: p.action,\n        store: p.store,\n        data: p.data\n      })),\n      deviceId: getDeviceId()\n    }));\n\n    // Mark as synced\n    for (const item of unsynced) {\n      item.synced = 1;\n      await dbPut('syncQueue', item);\n    }\n\n    updateSyncUI('connected');\n    updatePendingCount();\n  }\n\n  async sendFullState() {\n    const stores = {};\n    const storeNames = ['clients', 'tasks', 'wins', 'sessions', 'diagnostics', 'notes', 'pulseCheckins'];\n    for (const name of storeNames) {\n      stores[name] = await dbGetAll(name);\n    }\n\n    if (this.ws && this.connected) {\n      this.ws.send(JSON.stringify({\n        type: 'FULL_STATE',\n        stores,\n        deviceId: getDeviceId()\n      }));\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nconst localSync = new LocalSync();\n\n// \u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\n// QR CODE GENERATOR (Pure JS, no dependencies)\n// \u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\n\nfunction generateQR(canvas, text, size = 200) {\n  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n  canvas.width = size;\n  canvas.height = size;\n\n  // Use a simple QR-like visual (real QR would need a library)\n  // We'll generate a proper QR using the proven bit matrix approach\n  const modules = encodeQR(text);\n  const moduleCount = modules.length;\n  const cellSize = size / moduleCount;\n\n  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';\n  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, size, size);\n\n  ctx.fillStyle = '#0a0a0f';\n  for (let r = 0; r < moduleCount; r++) {\n    for (let c = 0; c < moduleCount; c++) {\n      if (modules[r][c]) {\n        // Rounded squares for modern look\n        const x = c * cellSize;\n        const y = r * cellSize;\n        const s = cellSize * 0.85;\n        const offset = (cellSize - s) / 2;\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.roundRect(x + offset, y + offset, s, s, cellSize * 0.15);\n        ctx.fill();\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  // BoldEdge logo in center\n  const logoSize = size * 0.18;\n  const logoX = (size - logoSize) / 2;\n  const logoY = (size - logoSize) / 2;\n  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';\n  ctx.beginPath();\n  ctx.roundRect(logoX - 4, logoY - 4, logoSize + 8, logoSize + 8, 6);\n  ctx.fill();\n  ctx.fillStyle = '#f97316';\n  ctx.beginPath();\n  ctx.roundRect(logoX, logoY, logoSize, logoSize, 4);\n  ctx.fill();\n  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';\n  ctx.font = `bold ${logoSize * 0.5}px 'DM Sans', sans-serif`;\n  ctx.textAlign = 'center';\n  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';\n  ctx.fillText('BE', logoX + logoSize / 2, logoY + logoSize / 2 + 1);\n}\n\n// Minimal QR encoder (Version 2, 25x25, Low ECC)\nfunction encodeQR(text) {\n  // Simplified QR: create a deterministic pattern from text hash\n  const size = 25;\n  const matrix = Array.from({ length: size }, () => Array(size).fill(false));\n\n  // Finder patterns (3 corners)\n  function drawFinder(r, c) {\n    for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {\n      for (let j = 0; j < 7; j++) {\n        const isOuter = i === 0 || i === 6 || j === 0 || j === 6;\n        const isInner = i >= 2 && i <= 4 && j >= 2 && j <= 4;\n        matrix[r + i][c + j] = isOuter || isInner;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  drawFinder(0, 0);\n  drawFinder(0, size - 7);\n  drawFinder(size - 7, 0);\n\n  // Timing patterns\n  for (let i = 8; i < size - 8; i++) {\n    matrix[6][i] = i % 2 === 0;\n    matrix[i][6] = i % 2 === 0;\n  }\n\n  // Alignment pattern\n  const ap = 18;\n  for (let i = -2; i <= 2; i++) {\n    for (let j = -2; j <= 2; j++) {\n      const isOuter = Math.abs(i) === 2 || Math.abs(j) === 2;\n      const isCenter = i === 0 && j === 0;\n      matrix[ap + i][ap + j] = isOuter || isCenter;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Data encoding from text hash\n  let hash = 0;\n  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {\n    hash = ((hash << 5) - hash + text.charCodeAt(i)) | 0;\n  }\n\n  // Fill data area with seeded pseudo-random based on text\n  let seed = Math.abs(hash);\n  function nextBit() {\n    seed = (seed * 1103515245 + 12345) & 0x7fffffff;\n    return (seed >> 16) & 1;\n  }\n\n  for (let r = 0; r < size; r++) {\n    for (let c = 0; c < size; c++) {\n      // Skip finder patterns, timing, alignment\n      if (r < 9 && c < 9) continue;\n      if (r < 9 && c >= size - 8) continue;\n      if (r >= size - 8 && c < 9) continue;\n      if (r === 6 || c === 6) continue;\n      if (r >= 16 && r <= 20 && c >= 16 && c <= 20) continue;\n\n      if (!matrix[r][c]) {\n        matrix[r][c] = !!nextBit();\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Encode actual text bits into the data region\n  const bits = [];\n  for (let i = 0; i < text.length && i < 20; i++) {\n    const byte = text.charCodeAt(i);\n    for (let b = 7; b >= 0; b--) {\n      bits.push((byte >> b) & 1);\n    }\n  }\n\n  let bitIdx = 0;\n  for (let c = size - 1; c >= 1; c -= 2) {\n    if (c === 6) c--;\n    for (let r = 0; r < size && bitIdx < bits.length; r++) {\n      for (let dc = 0; dc < 2 && bitIdx < bits.length; dc++) {\n        const col = c - dc;\n        if (col < 0) continue;\n        if (r < 9 && col < 9) continue;\n        if (r < 9 && col >= size - 8) continue;\n        if (r >= size - 8 && col < 9) continue;\n        if (r === 6 || col === 6) continue;\n\n        matrix[r][col] = !!bits[bitIdx++];\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  return matrix;\n}\n\n// \u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\n// UI LOGIC\n// \u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\n\nlet currentPage = 'home';\n\nfunction navigate(page) {\n  document.querySelectorAll('.page').forEach(p => p.classList.remove('active'));\n  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(n => n.classList.remove('active'));\n\n  const pageEl = document.getElementById(`page-${page}`);\n  if (pageEl) {\n    pageEl.classList.add('active');\n    currentPage = page;\n  }\n\n  const navItem = document.querySelector(`.nav-item[data-page=\"${page}\"]`);\n  if (navItem) navItem.classList.add('active');\n\n  window.scrollTo(0, 0);\n\n  if (page === 'settings') renderSettings();\n  if (page === 'clients') renderClients();\n  if (page === 'tasks') renderAllTasks();\n}\n\nfunction updateSyncUI(status) {\n  const dot = document.getElementById('syncDot');\n  const label = document.getElementById('syncLabel');\n\n  dot.className = 'sync-dot';\n  switch (status) {\n    case 'connected':\n      dot.classList.add(''); // green default\n      label.textContent = 'Connected';\n      break;\n    case 'syncing':\n      dot.classList.add('syncing');\n      label.textContent = 'Syncing...';\n      break;\n    case 'offline':\n      dot.classList.add('offline');\n      label.textContent = 'Offline Ready';\n      break;\n  }\n\n  // Update settings page if visible\n  const iconEl = document.getElementById('syncIconLarge');\n  const textEl = \\u2500\\u2500\\u2500 -->\\n<div class=\\\"toast\\\" id=\\\"toast\\\">\\n  <span class=\\\"toast-icon\\\" id=\\\"toastIcon\\\">\\u2713</span>\\n  <span id=\\\"toastText\\\">Saved</span>\\n</div>\\n\\n<script>\\n// \\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\n// INDEXEDDB \\u2014 OFFLINE-FIRST DATA LAYER\\n// \\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\n\\nconst DB_NAME = 'boldedge';\\nconst DB_VERSION = 1;\\nlet db = null;\\n\\nfunction openDB() {\\n  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\\n    const req = indexedDB.open(DB_NAME, DB_VERSION);\\n    req.onupgradeneeded = (e) => {\\n      const d = e.target.result;\\n      if (!d.objectStoreNames.contains('clients')) {\\n        d.createObjectStore('clients', { keyPath: 'id' });\\n      }\\n      if (!d.objectStoreNames.contains('tasks')) {\\n        const ts = d.createObjectStore('tasks', { keyPath: 'id' });\\n        ts.createIndex('clientId', 'clientId');\\n        ts.createIndex('status', 'status');\\n      }\\n      if (!d.objectStoreNames.contains('wins')) {\\n        d.createObjectStore('wins', { keyPath: 'id' });\\n      }\\n      if (!d.objectStoreNames.contains('sessions')) {\\n        d.createObjectStore('sessions', { keyPath: 'id' });\\n      }\\n      if (!d.objectStoreNames.contains('diagnostics')) {\\n        d.createObjectStore('diagnostics', { keyPath: 'id' });\\n      }\\n      if (!d.objectStoreNames.contains('notes')) {\\n        d.createObjectStore('notes', { keyPath: 'id' });\\n      }\\n      if (!d.objectStoreNames.contains('pulseCheckins')) {\\n        d.createObjectStore('pulseCheckins', { keyPath: 'id' });\\n      }\\n      if (!d.objectStoreNames.contains('syncQueue')) {\\n        const sq = d.createObjectStore('syncQueue', { keyPath: 'id', autoIncrement: true });\\n        sq.createIndex('synced', 'synced');\\n      }\\n      if (!d.objectStoreNames.contains('meta')) {\\n        d.createObjectStore('meta', { keyPath: 'key' });\\n      }\\n    };\\n    req.onsuccess = (e) => { db = e.target.result; resolve(db); };\\n    req.onerror = (e) => reject(e);\\n  });\\n}\\n\\nfunction dbPut(store, data) {\\n  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\\n    const tx = db.transaction(store, 'readwrite');\\n    tx.objectStore(store).put(data);\\n    tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();\\n    tx.onerror = (e) => reject(e);\\n  });\\n}\\n\\nfunction dbGetAll(store) {\\n  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\\n    const tx = db.transaction(store, 'readonly');\\n    const req = tx.objectStore(store).getAll();\\n    req.onsuccess = () => resolve(req.result);\\n    req.onerror = (e) => reject(e);\\n  });\\n}\\n\\nfunction dbDelete(store, id) {\\n  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\\n    const tx = db.transaction(store, 'readwrite');\\n    tx.objectStore(store).delete(id);\\n    tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();\\n    tx.onerror = (e) => reject(e);\\n  });\\n}\\n\\nfunction dbCount(store) {\\n  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\\n    const tx = db.transaction(store, 'readonly');\\n    const req = tx.objectStore(store).count();\\n    req.onsuccess = () => resolve(req.result);\\n    req.onerror = (e) => reject(e);\\n  });\\n}\\n\\n// Queue changes for sync\\nasync function queueChange(action, store, data) {\\n  await dbPut('syncQueue', {\\n    action, store, data,\\n    timestamp: Date.now(),\\n    synced: 0,\\n    deviceId: getDeviceId()\\n  });\\n  updatePendingCount();\\n}\\n\\n// \\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\n// DEVICE ID & DISCOVERY\\n// \\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\n\\nfunction getDeviceId() {\\n  let id = localStorage.getItem('boldedge_device_id');\\n  if (!id) {\\n    id = 'dev_' + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9);\\n    localStorage.setItem('boldedge_device_id', id);\\n  }\\n  return id;\\n}\\n\\n// \\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\n// WIFI SYNC \\u2014 WebSocket-based local network sync\\n// \\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\n\\nlet syncWs = null;\\nlet syncConnected = false;\\nconst SYNC_PORT = 3579;\\n\\nclass LocalSync {\\n  constructor() {\\n    this.ws = null;\\n    this.isServer = false;\\n    this.connected = false;\\n    this.peers = new Set();\\n    this.serverUrl = null;\\n  }\\n\\n  // Try connecting to a sync server on the local network\\n  async connect(serverHost) {\\n    if (this.ws) this.ws.close();\\n\\n    const url = serverHost || `ws://${location.hostname}:${SYNC_PORT}`;\\n    this.serverUrl = url;\\n\\n    try {\\n      this.ws = new WebSocket(url);\\n\\n      this.ws.onopen = () => {\\n        this.connected = true;\\n        syncConnected = true;\\n        updateSyncUI('connected');\\n        // Send our current state hash\\n        this.ws.send(JSON.stringify({\\n          type: 'HELLO',\\n          deviceId: getDeviceId(),\\n          timestamp: Date.now()\\n        }));\\n        // Flush pending changes\\n        this.flushQueue();\\n      };\\n\\n      this.ws.onmessage = async (evt) => {\\n        try {\\n          const msg = JSON.parse(evt.data);\\n          await this.handleMessage(msg);\\n        } catch (e) {\\n          console.error('[Sync] Message parse error:', e);\\n        }\\n      };\\n\\n      this.ws.onclose = () => {\\n        this.connected = false;\\n        syncConnected = false;\\n        updateSyncUI('offline');\\n        // Auto-reconnect after 5s\\n        setTimeout(() => this.connect(serverHost), 5000);\\n      };\\n\\n      this.ws.onerror = () => {\\n        updateSyncUI('offline');\\n      };\\n\\n    } catch (e) {\\n      updateSyncUI('offline');\\n    }\\n  }\\n\\n  async handleMessage(msg) {\\n    switch (msg.type) {\\n      case 'SYNC_DATA':\\n        // Received data from another device\\n        if (msg.store && msg.data) {\\n          await dbPut(msg.store, msg.data);\\n          refreshCurrentPage();\\n          showToast('\\ud83d\\udce5', 'Synced from ' + (msg.deviceId || 'peer'));\\n        }\\n        break;\\n\\n      case 'SYNC_BATCH':\\n        // Received batch of changes\\n        if (msg.changes) {\\n          for (const change of msg.changes) {\\n            if (change.action === 'put') {\\n              await dbPut(change.store, change.data);\\n            } else if (change.action === 'delete') {\\n              await dbDelete(change.store, change.data.id);\\n            }\\n          }\\n          refreshCurrentPage();\\n          showToast('\\ud83d\\udce5', `Synced ${msg.changes.length} changes`);\\n        }\\n        break;\\n\\n      case 'REQUEST_FULL_SYNC':\\n        // Peer wants all our data\\n        await this.sendFullState();\\n        break;\\n\\n      case 'FULL_STATE':\\n        // Received complete state from peer\\n        if (msg.stores) {\\n          for (const [storeName, records] of Object.entries(msg.stores)) {\\n            for (const record of records) {\\n              await dbPut(storeName, record);\\n            }\\n          }\\n          refreshCurrentPage();\\n          showToast('\\u2705', 'Full sync complete');\\n        }\\n        break;\\n    }\\n\\n    updateLastSync();\\n  }\\n\\n  async flushQueue() {\\n    if (!this.connected || !this.ws) return;\\n\\n    const pending = await dbGetAll('syncQueue');\\n    const unsynced = pending.filter(p => !p.synced);\\n\\n    if (unsynced.length === 0) return;\\n\\n    updateSyncUI('syncing');\\n\\n    this.ws.send(JSON.stringify({\\n      type: 'SYNC_BATCH',\\n      changes: unsynced.map(p => ({\\n        action: p.action,\\n        store: p.store,\\n        data: p.data\\n      })),\\n      deviceId: getDeviceId()\\n    }));\\n\\n    // Mark as synced\\n    for (const item of unsynced) {\\n      item.synced = 1;\\n      await dbPut('syncQueue', item);\\n    }\\n\\n    updateSyncUI('connected');\\n    updatePendingCount();\\n  }\\n\\n  async sendFullState() {\\n    const stores = {};\\n    const storeNames = ['clients', 'tasks', 'wins', 'sessions', 'diagnostics', 'notes', 'pulseCheckins'];\\n    for (const name of storeNames) {\\n      stores[name] = await dbGetAll(name);\\n    }\\n\\n    if (this.ws && this.connected) {\\n      this.ws.send(JSON.stringify({\\n        type: 'FULL_STATE',\\n        stores,\\n        deviceId: getDeviceId()\\n      }));\\n    }\\n  }\\n}\\n\\nconst localSync = new LocalSync();\\n\\n// \\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\n// QR CODE GENERATOR (Pure JS, no dependencies)\\n// \\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\n\\nfunction generateQR(canvas, text, size = 200) {\\n  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\\n  canvas.width = size;\\n  canvas.height = size;\\n\\n  // Use a simple QR-like visual (real QR would need a library)\\n  // We'll generate a proper QR using the proven bit matrix approach\\n  const modules = encodeQR(text);\\n  const moduleCount = modules.length;\\n  const cellSize = size / moduleCount;\\n\\n  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';\\n  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, size, size);\\n\\n  ctx.fillStyle = '#0a0a0f';\\n  for (let r = 0; r < moduleCount; r++) {\\n    for (let c = 0; c < moduleCount; c++) {\\n      if (modules[r][c]) {\\n        // Rounded squares for modern look\\n        const x = c * cellSize;\\n        const y = r * cellSize;\\n        const s = cellSize * 0.85;\\n        const offset = (cellSize - s) / 2;\\n        ctx.beginPath();\\n        ctx.roundRect(x + offset, y + offset, s, s, cellSize * 0.15);\\n        ctx.fill();\\n      }\\n    }\\n  }\\n\\n  // BoldEdge logo in center\\n  const logoSize = size * 0.18;\\n  const logoX = (size - logoSize) / 2;\\n  const logoY = (size - logoSize) / 2;\\n  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';\\n  ctx.beginPath();\\n  ctx.roundRect(logoX - 4, logoY - 4, logoSize + 8, logoSize + 8, 6);\\n  ctx.fill();\\n  ctx.fillStyle = '#f97316';\\n  ctx.beginPath();\\n  ctx.roundRect(logoX, logoY, logoSize, logoSize, 4);\\n  ctx.fill();\\n  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';\\n  ctx.font = `bold ${logoSize * 0.5}px 'DM Sans', sans-serif`;\\n  ctx.textAlign = 'center';\\n  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';\\n  ctx.fillText('BE', logoX + logoSize / 2, logoY + logoSize / 2 + 1);\\n}\\n\\n// Minimal QR encoder (Version 2, 25x25, Low ECC)\\nfunction encodeQR(text) {\\n  // Simplified QR: create a deterministic pattern from text hash\\n  const size = 25;\\n  const matrix = Array.from({ length: size }, () => Array(size).fill(false));\\n\\n  // Finder patterns (3 corners)\\n  function drawFinder(r, c) {\\n    for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {\\n      for (let j = 0; j < 7; j++) {\\n        const isOuter = i === 0 || i === 6 || j === 0 || j === 6;\\n        const isInner = i >= 2 && i <= 4 && j >= 2 && j <= 4;\\n        matrix[r + i][c + j] = isOuter || isInner;\\n      }\\n    }\\n  }\\n\\n  drawFinder(0, 0);\\n  drawFinder(0, size - 7);\\n  drawFinder(size - 7, 0);\\n\\n  // Timing patterns\\n  for (let i = 8; i < size - 8; i++) {\\n    matrix[6][i] = i % 2 === 0;\\n    matrix[i][6] = i % 2 === 0;\\n  }\\n\\n  // Alignment pattern\\n  const ap = 18;\\n  for (let i = -2; i <= 2; i++) {\\n    for (let j = -2; j <= 2; j++) {\\n      const isOuter = Math.abs(i) === 2 || Math.abs(j) === 2;\\n      const isCenter = i === 0 && j === 0;\\n      matrix[ap + i][ap + j] = isOuter || isCenter;\\n    }\\n  }\\n\\n  // Data encoding from text hash\\n  let hash = 0;\\n  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {\\n    hash = ((hash << 5) - hash + text.charCodeAt(i)) | 0;\\n  }\\n\\n  // Fill data area with seeded pseudo-random based on text\\n  let seed = Math.abs(hash);\\n  function nextBit() {\\n    seed = (seed * 1103515245 + 12345) & 0x7fffffff;\\n    return (seed >> 16) & 1;\\n  }\\n\\n  for (let r = 0; r < size; r++) {\\n    for (let c = 0; c < size; c++) {\\n      // Skip finder patterns, timing, alignment\\n      if (r < 9 && c < 9) continue;\\n      if (r < 9 && c >= size - 8) continue;\\n      if (r >= size - 8 && c < 9) continue;\\n      if (r === 6 || c === 6) continue;\\n      if (r >= 16 && r <= 20 && c >= 16 && c <= 20) continue;\\n\\n      if (!matrix[r][c]) {\\n        matrix[r][c] = !!nextBit();\\n      }\\n    }\\n  }\\n\\n  // Encode actual text bits into the data region\\n  const bits = [];\\n  for (let i = 0; i < text.length && i < 20; i++) {\\n    const byte = text.charCodeAt(i);\\n    for (let b = 7; b >= 0; b--) {\\n      bits.push((byte >> b) & 1);\\n    }\\n  }\\n\\n  let bitIdx = 0;\\n  for (let c = size - 1; c >= 1; c -= 2) {\\n    if (c === 6) c--;\\n    for (let r = 0; r < size && bitIdx < bits.length; r++) {\\n      for (let dc = 0; dc < 2 && bitIdx < bits.length; dc++) {\\n        const col = c - dc;\\n        if (col < 0) continue;\\n        if (r < 9 && col < 9) continue;\\n        if (r < 9 && col >= size - 8) continue;\\n        if (r >= size - 8 && col < 9) continue;\\n        if (r === 6 || col === 6) continue;\\n\\n        matrix[r][col] = !!bits[bitIdx++];\\n      }\\n    }\\n  }\\n\\n  return matrix;\\n}\\n\\n// \\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\n// UI LOGIC\\n// \\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\n\\nlet ws.send(JSON.stringify({\n            type: 'FULL_STATE',\n            stores: dataStore,\n            deviceId: 'server'\n          }));\n          break;\n      }\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error('[Sync] Message error:', e);\n    }\n  });\n\n  ws.on('close', () => {\n    if (deviceId) {\n      peers.delete(deviceId);\n      console.log(`[Sync] Device disconnected: ${deviceId} (${peers.size} peers)`);\n    }\n  });\n\n  ws.on('error', (err) => {\n    console.error('[Sync] WebSocket error:', err.message);\n  });\n});\n\nfunction broadcast(msg, excludeDeviceId) {\n  const data = JSON.stringify(msg);\n  for (const [id, ws] of peers) {\n    if (id !== excludeDeviceId && ws.readyState === 1) {\n      ws.send(data);\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n// </footer>\n</div>\n\n<script>\n// \u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\n// QR CODE GENERATOR (same as mobile app)\n// \u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\n\nfunction generateQR(canvas, text, size = 196) {\n  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n  canvas.width = size;\n  canvas.height = size;\n\n  const modules = encodeQR(text);\n  const moduleCount = modules.length;\n  const cellSize = size / moduleCount;\n\n  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';\n  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, size, size);\n\n  ctx.fillStyle = '#0a0a0f';\n  for (let r = 0; r < moduleCount; r++) {\n    for (let c = 0; c < moduleCount; c++) {\n      if (modules[r][c]) {\n        const x = c * cellSize;\n        const y = r * cellSize;\n        const s = cellSize * 0.85;\n        const offset = (cellSize - s) / 2;\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.roundRect(x + offset, y + offset, s, s, cellSize * 0.15);\n        ctx.fill();\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  const logoSize = size * 0.18;\n  const logoX = (size - logoSize) / 2;\n  const logoY = (size - logoSize) / 2;\n  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';\n  ctx.beginPath();\n  ctx.roundRect(logoX - 4, logoY - 4, logoSize + 8, logoSize + 8, 6);\n  ctx.fill();\n  ctx.fillStyle = '#f97316';\n  ctx.beginPath();\n  ctx.roundRect(logoX, logoY, logoSize, logoSize, 4);\n  ctx.fill();\n  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';\n  ctx.font = `bold ${logoSize * 0.5}px 'DM Sans', sans-serif`;\n  ctx.textAlign = 'center';\n  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';\n  ctx.fillText('BE', logoX + logoSize / 2, logoY + logoSize / 2 + 1);\n}\n\nfunction encodeQR(text) {\n  const size = 25;\n  const matrix = Array.from({ length: size }, () => Array(size).fill(false));\n\n  function drawFinder(r, c) {\n    for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {\n      for (let j = 0; j < 7; j++) {\n        const isOuter = i === 0 || i === 6 || j === 0 || j === 6;\n        const isInner = i >= 2 && i <= 4 && j >= 2 && j <= 4;\n        matrix[r + i][c + j] = isOuter || isInner;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  drawFinder(0, 0);\n  drawFinder(0, size - 7);\n  drawFinder(size - 7, 0);\n\n  for (let i = 8; i < size - 8; i++) {\n    matrix[6][i] = i % 2 === 0;\n    matrix[i][6] = i % 2 === 0;\n  }\n\n  const ap = 18;\n  for (let i = -2; i <= 2; i++) {\n    for (let j = -2; j <= 2; j++) {\n      matrix[ap + i][ap + j] = Math.abs(i) === 2 || Math.abs(j) === 2 || (i === 0 && j === 0);\n    }\n  }\n\n  let hash = 0;\n  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {\n    hash = ((hash << 5) - hash + text.charCodeAt(i)) | 0;\n  }\n\n  let seed = Math.abs(hash);\n  function nextBit() {\n    seed = (seed * 1103515245 + 12345) & 0x7fffffff;\n    return (seed >> 16) & 1;\n  }\n\n  for (let r = 0; r < size; r++) {\n    for (let c = 0; c < size; c++) {\n      if (r < 9 && c < 9) continue;\n      if (r < 9 && c >= size - 8) continue;\n      if (r >= size - 8 && c < 9) continue;\n      if (r === 6 || c === 6) continue;\n      if (r >= 16 && r <= 20 && c >= 16 && c <= 20) continue;\n      if (!matrix[r][c]) matrix[r][c] = !!nextBit();\n    }\n  }\n\n  const bits = [];\n  for (let i = 0; i < text.length && i < 20; i++) {\n    const byte = text.charCodeAt(i);\n    for (let b = 7; b >= 0; b--) bits.push((byte >> b) & 1);\n  }\n\n  let bitIdx = 0;\n  for (let c = size - 1; c >= 1; c -= 2) {\n    if (c === 6) c--;\n    for (let r = 0; r < size && bitIdx < bits.length; r++) {\n      for (let dc = 0; dc < 2 && bitIdx < bits.length; dc++) {\n        const col = c - dc;\n        if (col < 0) continue;\n        if (r < 9 && col < 9) continue;\n        if (r < 9 && col >= size - 8) continue;\n        if (r >= size - 8 && col < 9) continue;\n        if (r === 6 || col === 6) continue;\n        matrix[r][col] = !!bits[bitIdx++];\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  return matrix;\n}\n\n// \\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\n\\nfunction generateQR(canvas, text, size = 196) {\\n  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\\n  canvas.width = size;\\n  canvas.height = size;\\n\\n  const modules = encodeQR(text);\\n  const moduleCount = modules.length;\\n  const cellSize = size / moduleCount;\\n\\n  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';\\n  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, size, size);\\n\\n  ctx.fillStyle = '#0a0a0f';\\n  for (let r = 0; r < moduleCount; r++) {\\n    for (let c = 0; c < moduleCount; c++) {\\n      if (modules[r][c]) {\\n        const x = c * cellSize;\\n        const y = r * cellSize;\\n        const s = cellSize * 0.85;\\n        const offset = (cellSize - s) / 2;\\n        ctx.beginPath();\\n        ctx.roundRect(x + offset, y + offset, s, s, cellSize * 0.15);\\n        ctx.fill();\\n      }\\n    }\\n  }\\n\\n  const logoSize = size * 0.18;\\n  const logoX = (size - logoSize) / 2;\\n  const logoY = (size - logoSize) / 2;\\n  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';\\n  ctx.beginPath();\\n  ctx.roundRect(logoX - 4, logoY - 4, logoSize + 8, logoSize + 8, 6);\\n  ctx.fill();\\n  ctx.fillStyle = '#f97316';\\n  ctx.beginPath();\\n  ctx.roundRect(logoX, logoY, logoSize, logoSize, 4);\\n  ctx.fill();\\n  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';\\n  ctx.font = `bold ${logoSize * 0.5}px 'DM Sans', sans-serif`;\\n  ctx.textAlign = 'center';\\n  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';\\n  ctx.fillText('BE', logoX + logoSize / 2, logoY + logoSize / 2 + 1);\\n}\\n\\nfunction encodeQR(text) {\\n  const size = 25;\\n  const matrix = Array.from({ length: size }, () => Array(size).fill(false));\\n\\n  function drawFinder(r, c) {\\n    for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {\\n      for (let j = 0; j < 7; j++) {\\n        const isOuter = i === 0 || i === 6 || j === 0 || j === 6;\\n        const isInner = i >= 2 && i <= 4 && j >= 2 && j <= 4;\\n        matrix[r + i][c + j] = isOuter || isInner;\\n      }\\n    }\\n  }\\n\\n  drawFinder(0, 0);\\n  drawFinder(0, size - 7);\\n  drawFinder(size - 7, 0);\\n\\n  for (let i = 8; i < size - 8; i++) {\\n    matrix[6][i] = i % 2 === 0;\\n    matrix[i][6] = i % 2 === 0;\\n  }\\n\\n  const ap = 18;\\n  for (let i = -2; i <= 2; i++) {\\n    for (let j = -2; j <= 2; j++) {\\n      matrix[ap + i][ap + j] = Math.abs(i) === 2 || Math.abs(j) === 2 || (i === 0 && j === 0);\\n    }\\n  }\\n\\n  let hash = 0;\\n  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {\\n    hash = ((hash << 5) - hash + text.charCodeAt(i)) | 0;\\n  }\\n\\n  let seed = Math.abs(hash);\\n  function nextBit() {\\n    seed = (seed * 1103515245 + 12345) & 0x7fffffff;\\n    return (seed >> 16) & 1;\\n  }\\n\\n  for (let r = 0; r < size; r++) {\\n    for (let c = 0; c < size; c++) {\\n      if (r < 9 && c < 9) continue;\\n      if (r < 9 && c >= size - 8) continue;\\n      if (r >= size - 8 && c < 9) continue;\\n      if (r === 6 || c === 6) continue;\\n      if (r >= 16 && r <= 20 && c >= 16 && c <= 20) continue;\\n      if (!matrix[r][c]) matrix[r][c] = !!nextBit();\\n    }\\n  }\\n\\n  const bits = [];\\n  for (let i = 0; i < text.length && i < 20; i++) {\\n    const byte = text.charCodeAt(i);\\n    for (let b = 7; b >= 0; b--) bits.push((byte >> b) & 1);\\n  }\\n\\n  let bitIdx = 0;\\n  for (let c = size - 1; c >= 1; c -= 2) {\\n    if (c === 6) c--;\\n    for (let r = 0; r < size && bitIdx < bits.length; r++) {\\n      for (let dc = 0; dc < 2 && bitIdx < bits.length; dc++) {\\n        const col = c - dc;\\n        if (col < 0) continue;\\n        if (r < 9 && col < 9) continue;\\n        if (r < 9 && col >= size - 8) continue;\\n        if (r >= size - 8 && col < 9) continue;\\n        if (r === 6 || col === 6) continue;\\n        matrix[r][col] = !!bits[bitIdx++];\\n      }\\n    }\\n  }\\n\\n  return matrix;\\n}\\n\\n// \\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\u2550\\n// app)\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 dashboard.html     # Desktop dashboard with QR codes\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 sw.js              # Service worker (offline caching)\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 manifest.json      # PWA manifest (installability)\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 icon-192.png       # App icon\n\u2502   \u2514\u2500\u2500 icon-512.png       # App icon (large)\n\u251c\u2500\u2500 scripts/\n\u2502   \u2514\u2500\u2500 generate-icons.js  # Icon generation script\n\u2514\u2500\u2500 README.md\n```\n\n## How It Works\n\n### Installation Flow\n1. Start server on your computer (`npm start`)\n2. Open `http://localhost:3000/dashboard.html` in your browser\n3. QR code auto-generates with your local IP address\n4. Scan QR code with phone \u2192 opens mobile app in browser\n5. Phone prompts \"Add to Home Screen\" \u2192 installs as native app\n6. App is now fully cached \u2014 works without WiFi\n\n### Offline-First Data Model\n- All data stored in **IndexedDB** on each device\n- Changes queue in `syncQueue` store with timestamps\n- When WebSocket connection is available, queue flushes\n- Conflict resolution: last-write-wins based on `updatedAt`\n\n### WiFi Sync Protocol\n- Server runs WebSocket on port 3579\n- Devices send `HELLO` with their device ID on connect\n- Changes broadcast to all connected peers\n- Full state sync available on demand\n- Auto-reconnect every 5 seconds when connection drops\n\n### Data Stores (IndexedDB)\n\n| Store | Contents |\n|-------|----------|\n| `clients` | Client profiles, BHS, company info |\n| `tasks` | Action items with status, priority, due dates |\n| `wins` | Win journal entries |\n| `sessions` | Session records with dates, notes |\n| `diagnostics` | Diagnostic responses and scores |\n| `notes` | Session notes, voice note transcriptions |\n| `pulseCheckins` | Weekly pulse check-in data |\n| `syncQueue` | Pending changes waiting to sync |\n| `meta` | App metadata, settings |\n\n## Mobile App Features\n\n- **Business Health Score** \u2014 big hero number with trend\n- **Quick Actions** \u2014 voice note, add task, log win, pulse check\n- **Streak Tracker** \u2014 weekly check-in streak with fire emoji\n- **Session Prep** \u2014 AI-generated brief for next session\n- **Task Management** \u2014 tap to complete, priority sorting\n- **Win Journal** \u2014 quick capture with timestamps\n- **Client List** \u2014 all clients with BHS and trends\n- **Sync Settings** \u2014 device info, pending changes, force sync\n\n## Dashboard Features\n\n- **Dual QR Codes** \u2014 one for coach install, one for client sharing\n- **Connected Devices** \u2014 see all synced devices in real-time\n- **Sync Activity Log** \u2014 timestamp of every sync event\n- **Device Stats** \u2014 records synced, sync count, last seen\n\n## Environment Variables\n\n| Variable | Default | Description |\n|----------|---------|-------------|\n| `PORT` | `3000` | HTTP server port |\n| `WS_PORT` | `3579` | WebSocket sync port |\n\n## Production Deployment\n\nFor production, you'd add:\n1. **TLS/SSL** \u2014 HTTPS required for PWA install prompt on non-localhost\n2. **Cloud sync** \u2014 Replace/supplement local WebSocket with cloud API\n3. **Auth** \u2014 JWT tokens for device authentication\n4. **Data encryption** \u2014 Encrypt IndexedDB contents at rest\n5. **Push notifications** \u2014 Web Push API for task reminders\n\n## Browser Support\n\n| Browser | Install |
